# Mushoku Tensei: Jobless Reincarnation Lorebook

A comprehensive lorebook compiled from Mushoku Tensei wiki (mushokutensei.fandom.com), TVTropes character pages, and related reference sites.

Entries are organized under four main categories: Characters, Locations, World Lore, Items. Cross-references between entries (family ties, faction memberships, locations of residence) are woven into the entry descriptions themselves and not expressed as grouping sub-headings.

**Timeline Marking Convention**

Throughout the lorebook, timeline markers indicate when information is true. Markers used:

- `[Age 7-10]`, `[Age 12-16]`, etc.: Rudeus's age and corresponding life stage
- `[Pre-Metastasis]` / `[Post-Metastasis]`: before/after the Teleport Incident that scattered people across the world
- `[Pre-War]` / `[Post-War]`: before/after the Laplace-related war events near the end of the series
- `[Pre-Story]` / `[Laplace Era]` / `[Rudeus Era]` / `[Post-Death of Rudeus]`: for immortal figures and gods
- `[Demon Continent Journey]`: travel-phase marker used alongside `[Age 10-13: Demon Continent]`
- `[Early Timeline]` / `[Late Timeline]`: for general life-stage references

These markers are dynamic: the lorebook can be queried against a specific timeline point to determine what is currently true for a character or location.

**Entry Format Conventions**

- **Character cards**: `SHORT_DESC` (max 250 words) and `PHYSIQUE` (max 100 words). Punctuation: `|` new beat, `;` continues same beat, `:` expands a beat, `,` connects sub-details. The em-dash and "x, not y" / "considers it x rather than y" constructions are forbidden.
- **Location entries**: `DESC`, `MAP` (sub-locations), `NEARBY_LOC`, `POSSIBLE_CAST`. Empty fields are left blank.
- **Other lore** (races, magic, items, world info): simple `DESC` plus category-specific uniform fields.

# Characters

# Rudeus Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Reincarnated 34-year-old Japanese NEET [Birth: K401] reborn into a stillborn Laplace-Factor body, his soul transferred after his past life saved three high schoolers (including Nanahoshi Shizuka) from a truck at the cost of his own; in his previous life, became a Hikikomori after high school bullies publicly humiliated him by stripping him naked, tying him to the school gate, and showing his pictures to the entire school, called "limp dick"; the trauma kept him locked inside his room for nearly 20 years until his siblings kicked him out after their parents' funeral; cynical, intelligent, somewhat arrogant, plagued by self-loathing, determined to make his second life count; the novel follows his entire life from rebirth to peaceful passing at 74; [Age 7-10] Notos Greyrat magician, voiceless-incantation prodigy under Roxy; [Age 10-13] earned the nickname "Quagmire" from his signature Quagmire spell that immobilizes opponents in mud, Dead End co-founder; [Age 14-16] Ranoa student; [Age 17+] 7th of the Seven Great World Powers, Right Hand of the Dragon God, Education God, Rudo Mercenary Company president; [Epilogue: Age 74] dies of old age| [Age 7-10] humble to the point of discomforting others, terrified of reverting to past-life arrogance; legs tremble at stronger opponents, prefers to talk his way out; usually an egotistical person whose choices involving hurt pride caused his previous life's ruin and the Bad Future's ruin; despite his horndog nature, refuses to sleep with other women after marriage, even when his third wife Eris openly offers him the household beastkin servant Linia as a mistress; [Age 10-13] blood-lusting when his family is threatened; [Age 12-15] impotent and depressed after Eris abandons him; her letter failing to explain her reason caused erectile dysfunction that ruined any relationship for three years until Sylphie cured him; [Post-Sylphy marriage: Age 16-17] cured; perverted toward large-breasted and animal-eared women, never outside his three wives| Son of Paul and Zenith; first husband of Sylphiette, second of Roxy, third of Eris, openly defying Millis monogamy doctrine; suspicious of Man-God from the first dream and reluctant to follow his advice, at ease with Orsted despite Eris and Ruijerd being paralyzed in fear; [Post-Orsted fight: Age 11] subordinate of Orsted against Hitogami, previously a false dream-mentor; initially sided with Man-God against Orsted until his future self's diary proved Man-God was Evil All Along; Sylphie, Eris, and Nanahoshi all depend on him: Paul had to force Sylphie apart from him as she was becoming too dependent; Eris realizes she must become stronger to protect him in turn; in the bad timeline where he becomes obsessed with revenge, Nanahoshi kills herself as no one else could help her find a way home| Wears a lecherous smile while groping his wives in public; vomits under grief; declared kill-rats-on-sight policy after Hitogami's infected-rat plot against Roxy; modern-morals stance against incest strained when his half-sister Aisha eloped with his son Ars; his opposition to Aisha-Ars was based on his modern Japanese morals and the ghosts of his past attraction to his own niece, and he was never able to articulate why it was wrong to a society that did not see it as sinful, with even his second wife Roxy siding against him| [Age 5-7: Roa] tutor to Eris; [Age 10: Metastasis] teleported with Eris to Demon Continent; [Age 10-13] Dead End with Eris and Ruijerd, dies to Orsted, resurrected by Kishirika; [Age 13] meets Hitogami in dreams; [Age 14-16] reunites with Sylphiette as Fitz, marries her; when Zenith was willing to send the pregnant Lilia back to her hometown in bad weather right after giving birth, Rudeus accused Paul of raping Lilia to save Lilia's life, Zenith seeing through it and agreeing to let Lilia stay; after Sara slaps him for his erectile dysfunction, tried to stab himself in the throat, Soldat Heckler kicked the knife away and invited him to join his party; [Age 19-20: Begaritt] loses Paul and left hand to Manatite Hydra, rescues Zenith, marries Roxy; [Age 20] marries Eris; [Age 21-22] Zariff Prosthetic Arm made by Zariff with Zanoba and Cliff's collaboration; later fully restored by Orsted's healing; [Age 23+] father of Lucy, Lara, Ars, Sieghart; extremely distant to most of his children due to his Orsted work, only Christina really knew him on a personal level, this distance contributed to him not stopping Aisha and Ars's relationship before it turned sexual; hid volume 29 of his diaries, which dealt with the Aisha-Ars elopement, in a secret lab away from the other volumes, the only one that never reached the Asuran Kingdom; [Epilogue] foresees his descendants alongside Orsted continuing the fight against Hitogami; [Age 74] dies of old age surrounded by family}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Age 7] 145cm small-framed weak musculature; [Age 12-15] 169cm leaner; [Post-Metastasis] after Eris's separation and resulting ED, redirects his energy into regular body training, with the dividends showing once he intensifies his exercise; [Academy Era] since he spends most of his time in obscuring robes or uniforms, it contrasts when undressed and shows off his shredded physique; [Age 16+] 175cm muscular; [Age 13-19] as a teenager, lets his hair grow out past his shoulders and ties it into a low ponytail; [Age 19-20: Begaritt] left hand severed at the wrist by the Manatite Hydra during the Teleport Labyrinth rescue of Zenith; [Post-Begaritt] Zariff Prosthetic Arm: a magical prosthetic frame made by Zariff (magic-tool craftsman of Sharia) in collaboration with Zanoba Shirone and Cliff Grimoire, with a Mana Absorption Stone in the palm; this Zariff frame became the foundation for the entire Magic Armor MK series; [Late Timeline] left hand fully restored by Orsted's healing magic; [Epilogue] frail, weathered| Penile: [Age 14+] average, intact| Skin: fair| Face: average; [All Eras] mole beneath left eye, [Late Timeline] regular perv on his three love interests who all end up in a polygamous marriage with him; [All Eras] ahoge sticking up on top of his head; [Epilogue] full beard| Hair: [Age 7-10] light brown ponytail like Paul's; [Age 11] fades white, restored; [Age 22-23] white again; [Epilogue] entirely white| Eyes: [Birth] green; [Age 11] heterochromatic demon eyes by Kishirika, she crushes his right eye and replaces it with the Eye of Foresight that can see seconds into the future, [Age 11+] used to predict an opponent's next move but less effective against counter-attackers or faster/skilled opponents| Outfit 1 [Age 10-18]: gray traveling robes worn open| Outfit 2 [Age 20+]: refined clothing, Perugius-judged well-dressed}

# Sylphiette Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Half-elf magician of the Greyrat household, called "Sylphie" and "Silent Fitz"; doesn't have a surname until she marries Rudeus, thus joining the Greyrat family; quarter-Elven from her father's side, the remainder nearly all human, with a tiny portion of Beastman ancestry from her mother; given her grandmother is the notoriously promiscuous Elinalise, Sylphiette's father was looked down on as the product of one of her many dalliances, and so passed it onto Sylphiette, becoming one of the factors for her being shunned; born with bright green hair due to carrying the Laplace Factor, which produced the autosomal recessive trait; [Age 4-7: Buena Village] bullied by other kids in their village because of her non-human ancestry and green hair until Rudeus chased them off; Rudeus's childhood friend and first love; [Age 10-15: Post-Metastasis Ranoa] bodyguard to Princess Ariel under alias "Fitz"; [Age 16+ onward: Sharia] first wife of Rudeus, mother of Lucy and Sieghart; the emotional core of the Greyrat family, motivated to keep the household together, forgave Rudeus and Roxy for the affair during Zenith's rescue mission after Rudeus admitted infidelity because she understood he was vulnerable after his father's death; the most polite, kindhearted, and understanding of the three main heroines| [Age 4-7] shy, meek, dependent, cry-prone when separated from Rudeus; resolves to train alone when apart; [Age 10-15: Fitz persona] cold, silent, serious, intimidating, helpful, deeply insecure beneath; [Age 16+ onward] demure, supportive, accommodating, gradually more confident; gets huffy when Rudeus shows interest in other girls but does not oppose him taking Roxy and Eris as his wives because she understands they are special to him; thinks she isn't beautiful or buxom enough to attract Rudy after their reunion, especially because she's disguised as a boy for much of their relationship's development; demure and polite but is surprisingly open to admit she has erotic fantasies about Rudeus, even rambling in too much detail about her imagined love life to Ariel and Luke to her great embarrassment; absolute loyalty to Rudeus across all phases; manages the Greyrat household, the estate, the finances, and her husband's affairs while he is off helping Orsted; has a very vivid imagination and constantly has Imagine Spots about Rudy, her Crush Filter always shading him in a positive light| [Age 4-7] childhood friend and first love of Rudeus; [Post-Metastasis] secretly pining from afar at Ranoa; her troubles from Volume 7 through most of 9 are about her inability to tell Rudeus that she's his long-lost childhood friend Sylphie; her hymen blood stained the sheets when she gave her virginity to Rudeus, curing his erectile dysfunction; Rudeus stored the blood-stained sheets as a keepsake and later had them enshrined in the basement of his family home; [Post-Reunion: Age 16] wife of Rudeus, mother of Lucy and Sieghart; although Rudeus met Sylphie earlier, his first official girlfriend was Eris; after Eris leaves and triggers Rudeus's erectile dysfunction, meeting Sylphie again leads to them falling in love and marrying after she cures him; [Late Timeline] accepts any woman who truly loves Rudeus, defying Millis monogamy doctrine her mother followed; when she heard Rudeus say he was thinking about leaving their village to learn more about magic, Sylphie started crying and begged him to not go anywhere without her; later tells him she's more afraid of Rudeus disappearing on her than him having an affair; [Older Than She Looks] when Eris and Rudeus have become old, Sylphie looks like a woman in her thirties despite being the same age as Rudeus due to her elf heritage; was crucified and put on display after dying with Ariel and Luke in a failed Asuran coup attempt she joined after leaving the abusive alcoholic Rudeus; shows romantic interest in no one but Rudeus, in other timelines where he doesn't exist she never married; after her marriage, she quickly takes a backseat to the main plot, retiring from her bodyguard job and spending the rest of the story as Rudeus's housewife; the most feminine of Rudeus's love interests but still a royal bodyguard and a capable mage| Trained from childhood in voiceless incantation; has the Laplace Factor giving her bigger mana resources than average, but Orsted states that without training extensively she wouldn't have become that great; Wind and Water magic strong, Fire magic weak due to a childhood burn scar; better at healing than Rudeus who cannot chant for healing magic; able to use offensive and healing spells chantlessly; the "Gale Boots" allowing her to move at twice normal speed; sunglasses that are actually a magic item allowing her to perceive if Ariel is in danger; [Late Timeline] prone to quiet jealousy toward women near Rudeus yet never acts on it| [Age 4-7: Buena Village] bullied for green Superd-like hair and beast-race heritage, rescued by Rudeus, taught to read, write, use magic; [Age 10: Metastasis Event] teleported very high up in the air, cushioned her fall with magic by the skin of her teeth, used up so much mana her hair turned permanently white; [Age 10-15] disguised as boy knight "Fitz" to serve as Ariel's bodyguard, Rudeus thought Sylphie was a very pretty boy until he pulled her pants down, as Silent Fitz she became quite popular with the ladies; saved Ariel from a rampaging magical creature that killed her previous bodyguard Derrick; [Age 16-17: Reunion and Marriage] Rudeus didn't recognize her after the Mana Catastrophe because her hair color changed and she was posing as a boy, finally figuring it out when Sylphie recreated the situation of Rudeus stripping her like when he found out she's a girl in their childhood; revealed to Rudeus as Sylphy, cured his erectile dysfunction, married him, bore Lucy at age sixteen though having children at that age is considered normal in their world; [Late Timeline: Sharia] household wife and mother, retires from being Ariel's bodyguard once Ariel ascends the throne; her nickname "Sylphie" has also been translated as "Sylphy", her assumed name "Fitz" has been translated as "Fitts"}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Age 4-10] child frame; [Age 10-15] adolescent growth; [Age 16+] 162cm / 5'4", slender, ages slowly from elf heritage; A-Cup Angst over her petite figure, especially next to Ariel and Eris| Breasts: [Age 16+] B cup, modest| Vaginal Depth: [Age 16+] 5", Tightness: Average, bloodstained at defloration| Skin: pale| Face: very beautiful, elvish features| Hair: [Age 4-10] short green, Superd-like, a Mark of the Supernatural indicating her Laplace Factor; [Post-Teleport: Age 10 onward] white from mana shock as a result of exhausting her magic to survive being teleported midair, permanent in her early teens; [Age 10-15: Fitz era] cut short under the male disguise; [Late Timeline] grown long and feminine after marriage, styled in a more mature look| Eyes: red, inherited from her paternal grandmother Elinalise Dragonroad| Ears: long, indicators of elf ancestry, move up and down depending on her mood; she has the habit to touch her Pointy Ears when nervous or flustered| Outfit 1 [Age 10-15: Fitz era]: masculine academy uniform, white gloves worn permanently to hide identity, never takes off the sunglasses| Outfit 2 [Late Timeline: Wife]: feminine dresses, longer hair loose}

# Roxy Migurdia

SHORT_DESC: {Migurd magician; popular enough to headline her own spin-off manga, Mushoku Tensei: Roxy Gets Serious, narrating her life prior to meeting Rudeus; the only known Migurd to have been born without telepathy, treated similarly to being deaf because every other Migurd communicates telepathically and very few besides her and her parents can actually speak as they don't even bother to learn spoken language; her first daughter Lara did inherit the Migurd race's telepathic ability despite being only half-Migurdian; nearly 30 years older than Rudeus, mentally about the same age as him since Rudeus carries his past-life memories from birth; lifespan double of a human, body doesn't age past her early teens until she's at least 150 years old; before she met Rudeus, she had already been an adventurer for a couple of decades and traveled to many countries; [Demon Continent] Rikarisu Gang runaway, apprentice to Bloody Kant; [Ranoa] Water Saint under Jinas Halfas; left the Magic Academy because of a falling out with her mentor, who is now vice-principal and an acquaintance of Rudeus; [Buena Village] magic tutor to young Rudeus; also taught at the palace of the Shirone Kingdom for a few years while tutoring the seventh prince Pax; [Begaritt] rescued and married Rudeus after Paul's death; [Late Timeline: Sharia] Ranoa Magic Academy teacher, Water King Class, homeroom teacher of Rudeus's class, looks younger than her students; second wife of Rudeus, mother of Lara and Lily; blue-haired and the smartest of Rudeus's love interests; revered by Rudeus as his Goddess of Wisdom, Rudeus literally worships her for helping him overcome his agoraphobia from his previous life, her death in the alternate timeline driving him completely off the rails| [Lifelong] calm, collected, wise; always speaks very formally to highlight her real age and job as a teacher; pieces information together rationally; as a teacher at Ranoa Academy, tends to forget some things like lunches and schedules; clumsy and mistake-prone in tense situations, her targets for demonstrating magic are not well thought out; carries a complex about her petite childlike figure, persists across all phases since the Migurd body never ages past the 15-16 plateau; quite sensitive about her shortness; bristles when mistaken for a child; easily flustered and timid in social situations, especially around Rudeus after falling for him; has a hidden interest in sex, discovered by Rudeus when he spied on her listening in on his parents having sex, more willing to try new things in the bedroom than Sylphie after marriage| Daughter of Rowin and Rokari Migurdia; she and her mother look identical except for the hairstyles; [Buena Village onward] mentor to young Rudeus evolving into his romantic partner; [Post-Metastasis] searched the world for Rudeus and his family, formed a party with Elinalise and Talhand who were also looking for the family of their old fellow party members Paul and Zenith; idealistic romantic dreaming of a fateful labyrinth encounter modeled on Paul and Zenith's household; in all worlds where Rudeus does not exist, Roxy and Sylphie end up living together as friends for their entire lives| Rudeus literally worships her for helping him overcome his agoraphobia from his previous life; in the bad timeline where she dies, Rudeus becomes an alcoholic who massacres the Millis faith; although they had no bad intentions as both were looking for comfort after Paul's death, Rudeus still cheats on his first wife Sylphie when he has sex with Roxy, unlike his father Paul who did something similar with Lilia, Rudeus comes clean and asks Sylphie's permission to make Roxy his second wife; possibly gets the worst inferiority complex out of Rudeus's love interests because she effectively seduced him when he was depressed over his father's death despite knowing he already had a pregnant wife waiting for him, even considers herself more of a concubine than a wife, to Rudeus's perplexity; receives a new witch hat from Sylphie, who got it as a wedding gift after Roxy also became Rudeus's wife; her party arrived at a port town where Rudeus's own party is currently staying, but thanks to Man-God's intervention they never got to actually meet, even though she has a run-in with Eris and Ruijerd by accident, learning later from her parents about Rudeus's companions and giving her a Heroic BSoD| [Lifelong] sweet-toothed, greatly enjoys sweets, swore never to return to the Demon Continent for lack of sweets; dislikes green peppers, children, Superds (ironic given Ruijerd's friendship); turned to drinking after sweet alcohol in Kurasuma; eventually regretted not properly instructing Pax Shirone, who expected too much praise from her after she had mentored Rudeus and never praised Pax's efforts in learning magic; early in the anime it's a Running Gag that she's shown being groped by prince Pax at the Shirone Kingdom, Roxy then burns him with fire magic to make him stop; laments that her sudden weight gain from her first pregnancy may be permanent| [Childhood] born without Migurd telepathy, isolated within her tribe's silent conversation, ran away after seeing a visiting magician; [Demon Continent] joined Rikarisu Gang with Dale, Nokopara, Blaze; apprentice to Bloody Kant who gave her his hat after Dale's death; [Ranoa] graduated as Water Saint under Jinas Halfas, left without a word; [Buena Village] hired by Paul and Zenith as magic tutor to Rudeus for two years, lived with the Greyrat family for those years, told 5-year-old Rudeus to come back to her in 10 years when he was of age after he confessed his crush, due to 15 being considered adulthood in the Milis Continent; [Begaritt] trapped in the Teleport Labyrinth, slowly running out of food and mana, overwhelmed by the labyrinth's monsters, wets herself while facing what appears to be her imminent death, when she's saved she turns over to her handsome savior and introduces herself, not recognizing Rudeus because the last time she'd seen him he was a perverted little child, she doesn't recognize that this good-looking man in front of her is her former student until after he gives his name; rescued by Rudeus just before death; realizing that Rudy has surpassed her, she happily graduates him and heads off to develop her own skills further; [Post-Paul's death] slept with Rudeus to comfort him during his depression, became his second wife after Elinalise lied about Roxy being pregnant to force the issue; [Late Timeline: Sharia] teaches at Ranoa, after retiring from adventuring settles down to teach, still doesn't look older than 13 after mothering two daughters, looks only slightly older than her two daughters Lara and Lily, Lara could even be mistaken for her twin sister; raises Lara and Lily}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Lifelong] 142cm / 4'8", petite, looks like a cute young girl in her early teens but is actually a full-grown adult whose species ages slowly; childlike Migurd frame; appears 15-16 despite being 50+ years old, body does not visually change; the only one of Rudeus's love interests with an unchanging childlike body because her race doesn't age past their early teens until much later in their lives| Breasts: A cup, small| Vaginal Depth: 4", Tightness: Snug| Skin: sand-pale| Face: pretty, mature resting affect belying the body| Hair: [Lifelong] long blue, twin braids below the waist, makes her look somewhat childish on top of her young-looking body though she's a badass in the magic department; appears green in sunlight, mistaken for Superd green; matches her blue eyes and reflects her King-Class water magic, the specialty she practices from a toddler; hair and eyes both blue| Eyes: [Lifelong] blue, matching the hair| Outfit [Lifelong]: white and gray button-down shirt with black edges, short black skirt, light brown overlayer; [Bloody Kant period onward] Bloody Kant's hat, black with gold line and white side bands, beige robe and black witch hat; staff tipped with a blue gem, the Aqua Heartia / Arrogant Water Dragon King, eventually passed to Lara}

# Eris Boreas Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Swordswoman of the Boreas noble house of Asura, a noble daughter whose favorite thing is swordsmanship, the least feminine of Rudeus's love interests; called "Mad Dog Eris" and later "Mad Sword King" as her nickname in the Dead End party; from Classical Mythology: the mythological Eris is the goddess of strife and discord; the Boreas name itself evokes the Greek god of the North Wind, known for his strength and his temper, two qualities Eris is equally famous for; the most Hot-Blooded and short-tempered of Rudeus's love interests, the most tomboyish; protagonist of her own spin-off manga Mushoku Tensei: Eris Seriously Sharpens Her Fangs and an anime OVA; [Age 7-10: Pre-Metastasis Roa] violent noble failure under grandfather Sauros, first meets Rudeus and is so insulted that her tutor is younger than her that she slaps him in the face, then outright assaults him when he hits her back; drove off multiple tutors by the time Rudeus appears; loves to use this pose due to her love for Sauros, modeling herself after her grandfather; can't say almost anything without screaming, a habit she picked up from her grandfather; tutored by Rudeus and Ghislaine Dedoldia; [Age 10-13: Demon Continent] Dead End with Rudeus and Ruijerd; [Age 13-16: Holy Land of Swords] Sword Saint under Gal Farion; [Age 16+: Late Timeline Sharia] Mad Sword King, founder of Mad Sword God Style, third wife of Rudeus, mother of Ars and Christina; from a young age, proficient with the sword and gets better at it; attained Sword King rank and Saint rank in the North God Sword style, could have gone higher to Sword Emperor or Sword God but chose to focus on the Mad Sword God Style she founded; spends years training tirelessly at the Holy Land of Swords so she can become strong enough to protect Rudeus from Orsted; significantly improves over the journey; starts out as a loud Spoiled Brat but gradually softens| [Age 7-10] contemptuous, impulsive, defiant, short-tempered; shy underneath; [Age 10-13] raises her voice at anything new, bears fangs at threats, called the Mad Dog and noted as "free"; takes even the smallest trigger to send her into a violent rage, proves disadvantageous against Water God Sword practitioners who can read and react to her Killing Intent even before she makes a move; originally released bloodlust constantly, brought under control only after years of training; one of her biggest character developments is learning to control her temper, Nina helps her figure out how to turn off her Killing Intent by pointing out that she looks calmer and happier when she thinks of Rudeus; [Age 13-16] taciturn recluse ignoring anyone not speaking of Rudeus, swinging on a lonely peninsula for six months, not very academically inclined, by no means stupid but eventually forgot how to read and write after training solely under Gal Farion; very headstrong and straightforward, making it hard for her to express her feelings in sentimental words, struggles to say a simple "I'm here for you" to comfort Rudeus after his fight with Paul; [Age 16+] high libido, out-staminaed Rudeus in bed, considers sex as the only thing she's good at besides swordsmanship, Rudeus thinks she's his husband for the way she dominates him in bed, actually quite shy about exposing this part of herself to others besides Rudeus and was the hardest to convince when Rudeus wanted a foursome with all his wives; mellowing only with age; often seeking multiple servings, even if the food is no better than tolerable; among Rudeus's three wives she is the only full human, which is why only Eris ages at the same rate as Rudeus and eventually dies from old age before him; taken with beast girls Linia and Pursena, treating them as household pets, on some nights takes them into her bed and squeezes them tightly until she breaks their ribs| [Age 13: Abandonment] realized she was too weak to protect Rudeus from Orsted, gaining the resolve to grow stronger; left Rudeus a letter the morning after their first night to train at the Holy Land of Swords, an symbol of her resolve; the poorly-worded letter only said "they weren't a good match", Rudeus took it as her dumping him, becoming heartbroken and developing erectile dysfunction for three years; in the Bad Future this miscommunication led to Eris dying while protecting Rudeus before he learned her true feelings; the manga shows her crying after her first time with Rudeus, realizing she was relying too much on a younger boy; shortly after turning fifteen she returned to Fedoa only to find her parents and grandfather dead, that night she asked Rudeus to fulfill their promise to have sex two years earlier than planned to comfort herself and make him feel responsible so he wouldn't leave her; separates herself so she can become a woman worthy of him; Rudeus wakes up to find Eris gone with only most of her hair and a vaguely-worded letter left behind, he later burns the hair because carrying it was too painful; with the whole Boreas family dead, Eris was going to be sold as a concubine to a perverted nobleman, afterwards falsely reported as having died during the teleportation incident; [Age 16+: Reunion] returned to Rudeus as the saving him from Orsted's killing blow; after Orsted mortally wounds Rudeus in front of her, she cradles his dying body and screams for anyone to save him, Nanahoshi convinces Orsted to keep him alive; gets criticized twice after marrying Rudeus for her earlier abandonment, first by Sara then by Soldat, the second sticks when Soldat reveals Rudeus almost committed suicide; in her first fight against Orsted, she was utterly defeated and could do nothing but watch as Orsted mortally wounds Rudeus in front of her, motivating her years of training; married him, third wife, mother of Ars and Christina; Rudy and Eris are almost betrothed, Philip Boreas sent a letter to Paul just before Rudy's 10th birthday to set up the engagement, only the Mana Catastrophe prevented it from becoming official; she's one of Rudeus's love interests and eventual wives as well as his distant cousin; after she becomes an adult she's the most sexualized of Rudeus's love interests, the most buxom, and the best at the act of sex; as an adult has grown into an aggressive, blunt, and powerful warrior who enjoys swords and sex more than anything, Rudeus calls her the husband of their marriage; swordswoman to Rudeus's sorcerer, has beginner-level fire magic she uses for starting campfires or burning wax for letters; mostly uses fire magic for mundane purposes; trained under Ghislaine Dedoldia and Gal Farion; taught swordsmanship to Ghislaine's niece Minitona for the few months the Dead End party spent with the Doldia tribe; completely devoted only to Rudeus and never attracted to anyone else, in previous timelines married Luke but in current one violently rejects him because she only loves Rudeus; a heavy "harsh" type towards Rudeus, brash, prideful, and violent but occasionally open and affectionate with him, the only heroine with eyes slanted upwards| [Lifelong] relinquished the Boreas name after beginning training but keeps the Greyrat in her name as she still intends to marry Rudeus Greyrat; beast-race fetish runs in her Boreas blood, a family trait, once kidnapped a beastkin child from Dedoldia village because she thought she was cute, Rudeus was horrified and had to return the child; attempted to invoke this when she encouraged Rudeus to take Linia as a mistress during her pregnancy dry spell, insisted it doesn't count as cheating when a master slept with servants or slaves, Rudeus refused and kicked Linia out of the room| [Age 7-10: Roa] violent noble failure; Mana Catastrophe killed her family and broke her of all her old nobility habits, forcing her to re-evaluate herself; [Age 10-13: Demon Continent] panics at the sight of Ruijerd, a Superd, to the point of crying, gets over it after a few minutes of him not trying to eat her; Rudeus fakes a kidnapping to nudge her in the right direction, but the staged kidnapping becomes reality when one of the servants betrays them; her violent attitude as a child caused a servant to organize her kidnapping, she gets a brutal No-Holds-Barred Beatdown when she tries to escape; initially has a lot of pride due to being noble, looks down on Rudeus and refuses to take lessons from someone younger than her, becomes violent when he talks back, but Rudeus saving her from kidnappers humbles her; Rudeus moves into the Boreas House after being hired as Eris's Private Tutor and they live together for three years, gradually falling in love; her own father Philip planned to have her seduce Rudeus on the latter's tenth birthday after he showed reluctance to participate in the rebellion against the Notos's house, Eris isn't ready to go all the way yet; tells Rudeus to wait for his fifteenth birthday to go all the way; lost to Orsted, Rudeus nearly died; [Age 13-16: Holy Land of Swords] trained in the Land of the Sword God, which is relatively not far from Sharia City where Rudeus made his second home; defeated Jino Britts and Nina Farion; [Age 16+: Decisive Battle] chopped off Dragon God Orsted's arm, developed permanent white streaks from mana strain; [Late Timeline: Sharia] founding master of the Mad Sword God Style, bore Ars and Christina; [Epilogue] physically beat her son Ars when he would not stand up for himself after the family found out about his relationship with Aisha, accusing Ars of not protecting the woman he loves, even cutting off his arm after he tried to challenge her to protect the pregnant Aisha; [Age 74] after a regular day of training, Eris returned home tired and collapsed in bed, then passed away from old age}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Age 7-10] small girl, still developing; [Age 10-13] maturing, leaner from travel; [Age 13-16] athletic, toned, rock hard muscles and six pack abs; [Age 16+] 170cm / 5'7", athletic, voluptuous, firm, the most buxom of Rudeus's love interests with her mother's G-cup-size breasts| Breasts: [Age 16+] C cup developing into G cup, firm| Vaginal Depth: [Age 16+] 5.5", Tightness: Athletic| Skin: fair| Face: beautiful, sharp-featured, intense resting affect, prominently long canines when snarling| Hair: [Age 7-10] long red, worn loose; [Post-Decisive Battle: Age 16+] permanent white streaks from mana strain; [Late Timeline] red with white streaks persist| Eyes: red, matching her red hair| Outfit 1 [Age 10-13: Demon Continent]: traveling swordsgear with twin swords worn at both hips| Outfit 2 [Late Timeline: Sharia]: light casual wear at home, exposes her belly}

# Paul Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {S-Rank swordsman adventurer, a loving father who will do anything to protect his family and an experienced adventurer who in middle age will still go toe to toe with monsters; former head of the Greyrat household of Buena Village; a former adventurer turned border village knight; birth name Paul Notos Greyrat; of the Fangs of the Black Wolf party, an S-ranked adventurer group, and after the Mana Catastrophe forms the Fittoa Search Group to look for survivors from the Fittoa region; had his own share of adventures prior to Rudeus's birth, detailed in the Mushoku Tensei: Paul Gaiden mobile game scenarios supervised by the author; father of Rudeus, Norn, and Aisha; in all three main Sword Styles (Sword God, Water God, and North God); proficient in wielding two swords at once; handsome and quite ripped; when Rudeus first reincarnates he describes Paul as "a strong-looking, egotistical-looking man" with brown hair favorably compared to Japanese Delinquents, sure enough Paul is a Master Swordsman and a scummy Handsome Lech| [Age 12 onward] confident, bold, playful; speaks like a thug despite noble birth; downplayed but can be quite childish at times due to running away from home at age twelve; much more laid-back and hands-off as a parent than Zenith; in the early years, effectively the punchline of at least half of the Greyrat family's jokes; in Paul Gaiden Runaway Arc, not great in academic studies, only swordsmanship held his interest; [Buena Village era] caring family man with lingering womanizing; starts lackadasical and an open pervert, the Mana Catastrophe turns him into a Knight in Sour Armor, after reconciling with Rudeus mixes his seriousness with his old nature; [Post-Teleport] depressed, haggard, bloodshot, grown-out beard, drowning in alcohol to evade nightmares of failing his family; heard and saw many tragedies during the years-long search including people sold into slavery, gang raped, dead from falls, teleported into volcanoes; [Begaritt] desperate to rescue Zenith| Husband of Zenith under Millis Church's strict monogamy vow, the single-spouse doctrine scandalized by his affair with Lillia, forgiven by Zenith on Rudeus's intervention; second husband of Lillia; Zenith became pregnant after sleeping with him once which led both to retire from adventuring; his affair with Lilia also resulted in her pregnancy; father of Rudeus, Norn, Aisha; much closer to his mother Valentina than his father Amarant; son of Amarant Notos Greyrat; younger brother of Pilemon Notos Greyrat who took over the Notos family after Paul ran away; former lover of Ghislaine Dedoldia and Elinalise Dragonroad; never figured out that Rawls from Buena Village (and father of his son's childhood friend) is related to his ex-True Companions Elinalise until the latter told him herself; no one seems to like Paul: not the old Fangs of the Black Wolf party members, not the other Greyrats, not Zenith's family, even his wives Zenith and Lilia seem closer to each other than to him, only his daughter Norn holds him in high regard because the circumstances of her upbringing kept her from being exposed to his major flaws| Notos family fetish for large-breasted women inherited directly from his father Amarant (Rudeus inherits), a genetic disposition running through the entire Notos Greyrat line; a serial womanizer who bedded several female comrades before Zenith tied him down, before settling with Zenith, banged almost every woman who crossed his path; never faithful even after, bedding Lillia and openly flirting with Ghislaine; downplayed, prefers more active women especially in bed over nobles' daughters that wear too much makeup and don't exercise; developed a strong sexual interest in females at age 5; took advantage of Ghislaine during her beast-race estrus cycle, also forcefully took Lillia's virginity at the dojo and was expelled for it; was never really punished for raping Lilia as he ran away from the dojo after doing it and she forgave him and later seduced him herself; when his father Amarant attempted to call out his son for regularly putting his hands on the household servants and his skirt-chasing, Paul retorted that Amarant himself regularly slept with the maids; slept with his sword at his side even in bed, an adventurer's habit kept for life; kept his sword with money and adventurer's card in its sheath, which came in handy during the Mana Catastrophe; when Rudeus reunites with him, a frustrated Rudeus accuses Paul of having an affair with one of his female companions because he doubts Paul could resist putting his hands on a girl wearing such a Stripperiffic outfit, not knowing that girl dresses skimpily to keep men's stares away from her sister who was used as a Sex Slave; refuses to listen to his peers due to his hot-headed, obstinate, immature attitude and doesn't care about their perspectives; after Rudeus reunites with him, he accuses Rudeus of goofing off and ignoring what was at stake, gets angry at him for "vacationing" with Eris and "letting" Ruijerd do the heavy-lifting, until Geese breaks down how getting dropped into the Demon Continent isn't a walk in the park and makes Paul realize how badly he screwed up in expecting a kid dumped into the Demon Continent to handle more than an adult; quick to pass on all of his stories to his son Rudeus; fell for Zenith in the Fangs of the Black Wolf party and married her after pledging fidelity; even after marriage, still popular with the women around him; jokingly encouraged Rudeus to get with Eris when he sent him off to be her tutor, later subtly encouraged Rudeus to take Roxy as his wife too even though he already married Sylphie; was shocked that Rudeus told Zenith the affair was all his fault (technically Lilia seduced him) but went along with it to ensure Lilia and their unborn child wouldn't be forced out; knocks out Rudeus in an impromptu fight and ships him off to work for the Boreas family, recognizing that both Rudeus and Sylphiette became too co-dependent and stunt each other's growth; swordsman to his wife Zenith's mage; once tried to give Zenith a complete set of knight medals before they were married, was never able to complete the collection but she still loved it, Geese says he will complete Paul's collection for him after his death| [Childhood: Asura, Age 5] developed a strong sexual interest in females; [Age 10] lost his virginity to another noble girl, immediately took to skirt chasing; [Age 12] fought with father Amarant (who himself regularly slept with maids), threw away the Notos name, ran away to adventure, leaving his younger brother Pilemon to take over the family; his trouble growing up and socializing in nobility, especially the expectations placed on him as the firstborn son, fueled his Manchild tendencies; in Paul Gaiden, after Valentina became dreadfully ill and died, both Amarant and Pilemon blamed Paul for "murdering" her for not being there when she wanted to see him most; [Asura Adventurer era] formed Fangs of the Black Wolf with Zenith, Lillia, Ghislaine, Elinalise, Talhand; [Buena Village era] settled with Zenith, fathered Rudeus (K401), then Norn, then affair-child Aisha with Lillia; [Post-Teleport: K417] led the Fedoa Region Search Group across the world; [Begaritt: K419-420] fought the Manatite Hydra in the Teleport Labyrinth; while looking for Zenith on the Begaritt Continent, attacked by a succubus and fell under its hypnotic influence, [Post-Metastasis] pledged he would not sleep with another woman post-catastrophe until he had found Zenith, deeply ashamed when forced to break this vow under the succubus's influence that required Lillia to sleep with him to dispel; kicked Rudeus out of the way of the Hydra's attack, lost his lower half, dying instantly; the Hydra rips Paul's body in half; before he dies, has a faint smile as he sees and knows that Rudeus had survived the ordeal; died with his eyes open looking directly at Rudeus, his gaze expressing relief to see his son safe, which becomes a temporary trauma for Rudeus because every time he visualizes his father in his following depression it's with that dead stare; the first major character to die; "Pauro" (パウロ) as written in Japanese, can be translated as Paul or Paulo}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Age 12 onward] tall, muscular, handsome, broad-shouldered, scarred; [Post-Teleport] haggard, grown-out beard, drawn, Exhausted Eyebags; [Begaritt] ripped in half by the Hydra, decapitated by a surviving head| Penile: [Adult] above average, intact| Skin: fair, scarred from adventuring| Face: handsome, mole near the left eye (Rudeus inherits), a Lascivious Beauty Mark; [Post-Teleport] bloodshot, drawn| Hair: sandy light-brown, short framing the face with the rest gathered in a long ponytail over the shoulder; [Post-Teleport] unkempt| Eyes: green; [Post-Teleport] bloodshot| Outfit 1 [Pre-Teleport]: double-edged "Beloved Sword" with triangular crossguard, paired with a curved black short sword dual-wielded in the offhand, adventurer's tunic and boots; keeps money and adventurer's card in the sword sheath| Outfit 2 [Buena Village]: casual wear with the sword still slung at the hip}

# Zenith Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: { healer magician of the Greyrat household and a sweet, loving personality; blonde and a sweet woman as well as a good wife and mother; consistently tries her best to be a kind and caring mother to her children, Rudeus almost never critical of Zenith as a parent by comparison to Paul; second daughter of an Earl of the Holy Country of Millis; raised sheltered from the world by the Latriea household but chaffed under her strict parents; née Latreia; wife of Paul under the Millis Church's strict monogamy vow; mother of Rudeus and Norn; stepmother to Aisha; bearer of latent Miko telepathy manifesting only post-rescue; gains the power of telepathy as a result of being trapped inside a magic crystal for years, in return becoming mute (and unable to chant for her magic); according to Orsted's theory, the original "Rudeus Greyrat" was a stillborn baby whose body was a convenient vessel for the reincarnating soul| [Childhood: Millis] warm, devout Millis follower, model student nicknamed "Millis young lady standard"; [Age 15] rebellious, ran from an arranged noble marriage; [Fangs era] extremely naive, constantly taken advantage of by other adventurers who paid her chronically under the standard rate for an Intermediate-level healing mage, used as a human shield, until Paul basically kidnapped her into his own party and Elinalise convinced her of how she'd been used; [Buena Village era] warm, loving mother with a strict edge, prone to anger at Paul's womanizing and at anyone damaging her garden plants; [Post-Crystal: Sharia] dreamlike trance, near-expressionless, the underlying personality surfaces in flashes, smiling at Norn or striking Rudeus for announcing Eris as a third wife; a very traditional, religious, motherly woman from a noble family drawn to the wild, dashing, womanizing swordsman Paul, deconstructed as his womanizing comes back full force and he cheats on her| Wife of Paul under strict Millis monogamy vow scandalized by his affair with Lillia; well-endowed figure, her husband's preference; the first wife, wanted the marriage to remain monogamous; very angry with Paul when she finds out he cheated on her with Lilia while she was pregnant with Norn, but can't bring herself to kick Lilia out since she also got pregnant and with Rudeus's persuasion accepts Lilia as Paul's second wife; in worlds where Rudeus did not exist, Zenith always forgives Lilia but her marriage to Paul was never the same after his infidelity; Paul cheated with Lilia because Zenith was pregnant with Norn and Paul was abstaining from sex with his wife out of concern for the pregnancy; gets along well with the taciturn maid Lilia; mother of Rudeus, Norn; stepmother to Aisha; grandmother to Lucy, Lara, Ars; daughter of Carlyte and Claire Latreia; she's a skilled mage and her husband Paul is a swordsman, Paul wanted their son Rudeus to become a swordsman too but after they discovered his talent for magic, Zenith insists they hire Roxy to train him as a mage| Conservative Millis morals: the monogamy vow scandalized by Paul's affair with Lillia; passive disapproval of Rudeus's polygamy surfaces even from her trance, reacts badly to Rudeus taking multiple wives; loved Aisha like her own daughter, even nursing her, and directly told Lillia she considered Aisha her own daughter; slapped Paul immediately after finding out he cheated on her and got Lillia pregnant, slapped Rudeus again when he announced his intention to make Eris his third wife, and again when he acted antagonistically towards his strict grandmother not realizing her intentions; had Rudeus at age 17, Norn at age 22| [Childhood: Millis] never broke a Millis Church rule; [Age 15] rebelled against an arranged noble marriage, ran away to become a solo healer-adventurer; [Fangs era] conned and used as a shield until Paul saved her, joined his party as healer, learned to cook from Geese so she could become closer to Paul; [Teleport Incident: K417] trapped inside a magic crystal in the Teleportation Labyrinth at Rapan, kept ageless and dream-stupored until Rudeus, Roxy, and Paul freed her; [Post-Rescue: Sharia, K420+] left in a permanent near-catatonic state, mute as a side effect of being trapped in the crystal, the Blessed Child of Memory reveals she is still self-aware but her consciousness can only perceive her surroundings as a distant dream, only able to communicate telepathically with her granddaughter Lara and the sacred beast Leo; former adventurer turned ordinary housewife; unaffected by Orsted's curse}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 165cm / 5'5", slender, voluptuous; [Crystal Entrapment: K417-K420] appears ageless, frozen at age 28| Breasts: E cup, large, inherited by Norn| Vaginal Depth: 6", Tightness: Mature| Skin: fair| Face: beautiful, gentle resting affect; [Post-Rescue] placid, near-expressionless| Hair: blonde, ponytail with chin-length strands framing either side, with an ahoge at the crown inherited by both her children| Eyes: blue-shaded| Outfit 1 [Pre-Teleport]: light brown blouse, black ribbon, khaki belted skirt, white halterneck corset exposing the bust, black thigh-high boots| Outfit 2 : blue and white cleric's outfit with boob window and back cutout| Outfit 3 [Post-Rescue]: white nightgown flaunting ample cleavage}

# Lillia Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Guard-maid of the Greyrat household; Water God Style Intermediate-rank swordswoman; second wife of Paul after the affair; mother of Aisha; former royal guardsmaid of the Silver Palace of Asura; savior of the infant Ariel Anemoi Asura; the only member of the Greyrat family who wears glasses| [Silver Palace era] professional, stoic from guard-maid training, a permanent result of the training; rarely displays outward emotion; [Greyrat Household: Buena Village] thawing into fuller expression only after becoming Paul's second wife; [Post-Teleport: Shirone Kingdom] raises Aisha alone in foreign palace exile; [Late Timeline: Sharia] senior maid, quietly superstitious, believing in evil spirits and omens; [Epilogue] confesses to Rudeus she never wanted Aisha, viewing her as a constant reminder of her betrayal of Zenith, questioning why Aisha had ever been born during the year Aisha was missing| Second wife of Paul, his affair with her violated the Millis Church's strict monogamy vow binding him to Zenith; mother of Aisha; stepmother to Rudeus and Norn; grandmother to Ars via Aisha and to Leroy via Aisha-Ars| Trained as both domestic maid AND combat bodyguard, a "royal guardsmaid" hired as a disposable Human Shield; perfect maid, mediocre swordswoman who never reached beyond Intermediate rank in the Water God Style despite years of hard work, while Paul surpassed her quickly despite goofing off; the poisoned-dagger leg wound ended her court career with permanent slight paralysis and dismissal from the palace; [Paul Gaiden] her father planned an Arranged Marriage between her and one of his male disciples to name a dojo successor, Paul fitting the criteria by his swordsmanship talent, the attempt failed when Paul had different ideas; [Paul Gaiden] Rape as Backstory: Paul was expelled from their dojo after forcefully taking her virginity, she brushed it off because "Paul's not a bad guy", in hindsight her first time was better with him than with the fat depraved nobles at the palace; Zenith demanded Paul not sleep with Lilia unless Zenith was unable to, the rule respected until a succubus attack forced Lilia to sleep with Paul to break the succubus's influence on him; Saw Rudeus as her personal hero after he convinced Zenith to let her stay, raising Aisha to be his servant out of gratitude; at Rudeus's birth she saw him emerge with a totally emotionless face, initially assumed he was a stillborn, then concluded after witnessing his perverted behavior that he was possessed by a demon, in the roundabout truth the original Rudeus Greyrat was indeed a stillborn whose body became the vessel for a world-weary NEET's soul; declined to tell Sylphie where Rudeus went for his tutoring job, choosing to tutor Sylphie in manners and etiquette so the girl would become a proper lady when she reunited with Rudeus; Raised Aisha to potentially become Rudeus's wife, lover, or concubine, this grooming accidentally caused the Aisha-Ars elopement because Aisha was trained to "always satisfy her master"; Found out Rudeus stole Roxy's panties while cleaning, never revealed it to his parents, one reason Rudeus went to bat for her over the affair; Disciplines Aisha with a tap on the head when out of line; When Pax Shirone takes her hostage in the Shirone Kingdom arc, she is bound, gagged, and used to capture Rudeus; Cried grateful tears when Rudeus convinced Zenith to let her stay, and again when he rescued her from Pax Shirone; [Late Timeline] Jokingly offers Aisha to Rudeus after she grows up, teases him with Marshmallow Hell; [Epilogue] Aisha started a sexual relationship with Rudeus's son Ars at age 10, became pregnant, the two eloped; after Aisha was brought back, Lilia took a knife and tried to murder her own daughter, Rudeus stopped her and refused to kill Aisha; she only accepted Aisha-Ars marriage because Rudeus insisted the entire family stay together; her Shotgun Wedding with Paul in the main story becomes ironic given that, per Paul Gaiden, she was originally intended to be married to him when they were younger as part of her father's arranged-marriage plan to name a dojo successor; "Lilia", "Lilya", or "Lillia", multiple acknowledged transliterations| [Childhood: Southern Asura] daughter of a Water God Style dojo family, raised as a swordswoman; [Royal Service: Silver Palace] guard-maid to the newborn princess Ariel Anemoi Asura, took a poisoned-dagger slash to the leg protecting her, slight permanent paralysis, dismissed from court, the royal family could have executed her because she knew the palace layout, only fired, made herself scarce to avoid being silenced; [Greyrat Household: Buena Village] hired during Zenith's first pregnancy, seduced Paul while Zenith was pregnant with Norn by leaving her door slightly ajar, confessed pregnant with Aisha, Rudeus claimed Paul assaulted her to deflect Zenith's anger, forgiven by Zenith on Rudeus's intervention, Paul married her to legitimize Aisha; [Post-Teleport: Shirone, K417+] raised Aisha alone at the Shirone royal palace after Pax Shirone took her hostage; [Late Timeline: Sharia] senior maid, raises Aisha and Leroy; [Epilogue] reconciles with Aisha after Rudeus's intervention, accepts Aisha and Ars as a married couple}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 162cm / 5'4", slender, voluptuous; [Post-Court Dismissal] permanent leg injury from the poisoned dagger, causing a slight limp and the end of her full-strength running and stomping| Breasts: E cup, very large| Vaginal Depth: 5.5", Tightness: Mature| Skin: fair| Face: very beautiful, oval glasses perched at the bridge, the only Greyrat family member who wears glasses; rarely displays outward emotion| Hair: reddish-brown, tied in a half-bun with chest-length strands falling on either shoulder| Eyes: violet-purple| Outfit [Lifelong]: black dress with gigot sleeves, white apron, white waistband emphasizing the bust, white stockings, black strapped shoes, black-and-white hairband, oval glasses}

# Norn Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Student of the Magic University of Ranoa and writer; of the Ranoa Magic Academy; the crux of her childhood issues: while both her siblings are bona fide geniuses and child prodigies, Norn is only an average child, when she starts attending Ranoa Magic Academy everyone expects her to be as gifted as Rudeus, causing a mental breakdown and shut-in period; further exacerbated by Zenith's family who constantly put her down because she couldn't outperform what they saw as an "illegitimate child" Aisha; very clumsy especially compared to her siblings, acknowledged In-Universe, gets a fan club because of it; second child of Paul and Zenith; younger sister of Rudeus; elder twin by hours of Aisha; bearer of Greyrat and Latreia noble blood; niece of Claire Latreia; ends up really attached to her father as she spends a good part of her formative years raised by him alone; developed a crush on Ruijerd Superdia during his mission to escort her and Aisha to Sharia, eventually marrying him in a arranged by Orsted and accepted by Norn with no fight before she was even told who she was going to marry, to Ruijerd whose first wife Luwelia was killed when Laplace's curse drove him to kill his own family| [Teleport era: Age 4-10] as a child hides behind her parents when she's around strangers or people she distrusts, like Rudeus; independent, hardworking, prone to mistakes under pressure; strongly attached to her father, hating anyone who hurts him; her first impression of Rudeus was walking in on him punching their father, didn't know Paul started the fight, Rudeus was shipped off to start working for the Boreas family a year after she was born and the teleportation incident caused her entire family to be divided, leaving her with Paul, so from her perspective Rudeus was a major cause of Paul's grief, spending a good deal of her early childhood resenting him; loathed Rudeus after his fight with Paul, refusing reconciliation for years; inherited Paul's sword after his death, now a wall decoration in her bedroom; carries a serious inferiority complex from constant comparison to her outstanding siblings, severe enough that she nearly became a shut-in before her siblings pulled her through; gradually warms towards her brother once she and Aisha start living at Sharia City, Ruijerd's kindhearted nature won Norn over and was one of the reasons she decides to stay with Rudeus, Rudeus making an effort to reach out to her at her lowest point makes Norn finally open up to him because he respected her as a person, when she becomes a Hikikomori Rudeus goes to her room to talk, she finally accepts he isn't the abusive person she thought him to be and ends up hugging him and crying into his chest; [Late Timeline: Sharia] gradually heals, develops her writing talent; acts as an older sister figure to Rudeus's children, helped by the fact that she isn't much older than them; when Paul sends her and Aisha to live with Rudeus and Sylphie at Sharia City, Norn can't accept this decision and asks to live in a dorm by herself at age 10, Rudeus allows her on the condition she visit once a month| Younger sister of Rudeus; elder twin of Aisha by hours; niece of Claire Latreia; student of the Ranoa Magic University; with Aisha, opposite to her half-sister Norn is shy and talentless, and isn't fond of their older brother Rudeus at first; fell for the much older Ruijerd as a preteen and married him when she grew up; loves Ruijerd who is over 500 years older than her and first met her when she was a child; she is a human and Ruijerd is a Superd, their marriage occurred in all of Orsted's previous loops; was told by her mother that Superds were all scary but ends up falling in love with Ruijerd, even marrying and having a child with him; has feelings for Ruijerd because of his kind and protective nature, marries him when she's an adult| Devout Millis believer instilled by her mother's family; the Millis Church strictly requires monogamy, downplayed when Rudeus announces he wants to take Roxy as his second wife, Norn is the only one in the family who strongly opposes it because Rudeus is already married to Sylphie and as a follower of the Millis faith she can't condone polygamy, she still accepts Roxy because Sylphie doesn't protest; gets very upset with Rudeus when he admits he slept with Roxy when he was away from his pregnant wife Sylphie, outraged that Rudeus is sleeping with a girl who looks close to his younger sisters' age even though Roxy is actually older than Rudeus; slowly accepting the household over time; tends to ignore her father Paul's flaws while criticizing Rudeus for similar behavior, has to be reminded that no one else in the family besides her and Zenith are Millis followers and Paul also had two wives because he cheated on her mother; fell in love with Ruijerd at a young age and never felt attracted to any man besides him; eventually had a daughter with him; Man-God intends to harm Norn to break Rudeus after missing his only chance to kill Roxy (and Lara); when working on a joint project Rudy assigned, embarrassed when Julie calls her sensei| [Birth: Buena Village, K413] born hours before Aisha; [Teleport Incident: Age 4] teleported with only Paul, lived with him in Millishion and briefly with the Latreia family; [Late Timeline: Sharia, Age 10+] lives in Rudeus's household, attends the Magic University of Ranoa, develops her writing talent; eventually becomes Student Council President; [Adulthood] marries Ruijerd Superdia and bears his daughter}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Age 4-10] child frame; [Age 10-15] adolescent growth; [Late Timeline] 162cm / 5'4", curvaceous, often mentioned to look a lot like her mother Zenith, inheriting Zenith's figure| Breasts: [Late Timeline] D cup, large, inherited from Zenith| Vaginal Depth: [Late Timeline] 5.5", Tightness: Average| Skin: fair| Face: beautiful, resembling Zenith; has an ahoge like her mother and brother| Hair: blonde| Eyes: green| Outfit 1 [Teleport era: Age 4-10]: child's traveling clothes| Outfit 2 [Late Timeline: Ranoa]: Magic University uniform| Outfit 3 [Late Timeline: Sharia home]: casual dresses}

# Aisha Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: { illegitimate daughter of Paul and Lillia, brilliant from a young age, considered a genius because of her many talents but also raised by Lilia to believe she couldn't be more than a servant to the Greyrat family; her mother's upbringing and Rudeus's incapability to put his foot down unintentionally limited her potential: in every universe where Rudeus didn't exist, Aisha actually went to the magic academy and became the greatest mage of her generation, advancing magic by leaps and bounds, even figuring out how to extend human lifespans and using it to extend her own, living to be at least 200 years old, far past Orsted's loops, in the current timeline she died at 86 due to old age and high stress from constant work with Orsted who was aligned with the Ogre God's Empire, Orsted himself spent time lamenting how great Aisha was supposed to be but never achieved due to her constant service to her brother; younger half-sister of Rudeus and Norn by hours; a bright and outgoing little girl, her mother Lilia taught her that she should act cute if she wants men to do what she wants; household manager of the Greyrat home in Sharia; eventually becomes Rudeus's aide at the Rudo Mercenary Company, vice president; proves to be excellent at math, going along with her other excellent managerial skills; eventually partner to Ars Greyrat and mother of Leroy; creator of the spell that brought Akito Shinohara to the Six-Faced World, setting up his fateful meeting with the Blessed Child of Restoration that established the current timeline| [Childhood: Age 4-10] speaks and acts like an adult maid well beyond her age, Lilia raised Aisha to be a maid in place of a daughter of the Greyrat household, so she was educated to always speak very formally even when addressing her siblings; trained by Lilia from a young age to do housework; smart, manipulative, cunning, cute, ruthlessly indifferent to the cost her subjects pay under her schemes, once working Ruijerd to open complaint, manipulating Rudeus into yielding his side of any story, and slipping out of Castle Chaos Breaker alone; [Late Timeline: Sharia] rejoined Rudeus's household, perfect-scored the Magic University entrance exam at 10, was offered a scholarship for her academic skills alone but chose to stay as Rudeus's maid, manages the household's domestic operation| her mother raised her as a maid so she could serve Rudeus; subverted, technically legitimate since Paul married Lilia to ensure she'd be legitimate, but Lilia raised her to only see herself as a servant of the family in place of a member, and the Latreia family refused to recognize Paul's marriage to Lilia, treating Aisha as an illegitimate bastard child of a mistress; playful personality with a major brother-complex, occasionally offers herself to Rudeus although she's just teasing him for the most part, subverted when it is revealed she didn't actually want this to happen but accepted the possibility as part of her role as a maid, idolizes Rudeus for the rest of her life; one of the few people who's always loyal to Rudeus, in the bad future Oldeus timeline she was one of the few to never betray or abandon him alongside Perugius and Eris, the only time she didn't seek her brother's approval or heed his wishes was when she pursued a relationship with Ars; making suggestive comments toward Rudeus, undisturbed by nudity in his presence during shared baths; enjoys caring for plants and cultivates rice for Rudeus despite less favorable conditions at their home, manages to domesticate a baby treant; Rudeus had to allow Aisha to keep a treant as a pet lest she tells his wives about his Stalker Shrine at their basement; fond of gardening and cute things, both suppressed by her mother in favor of maid duties| carries an inferiority complex as the illegitimate daughter, convinced the family loves Norn more, hungry for attention and fiercely competitive with Norn; with Norn, opposite to her half-sister Aisha is cheerful and talented and adores Rudeus; their relationship is poor and worsened by step-grandmother Claire Latreia's open disdain at Aisha's outshining Norn, the Latreia family constantly demeaned Aisha despite her obvious intelligence, Norn is jealous of Aisha because she's smarter and more talented, in return Aisha is jealous of Norn because she's the daughter of Paul's Top Wife Zenith while Aisha is the child of his second wife Lilia; in the alternate timeline where Rudeus didn't exist, Aisha went to school and grew up to become a magical prodigy| [Epilogue] 14-year age gap with her half-nephew Ars, she helps raise Ars from birth and has a bigger attachment to him than Rudeus's other children, from the moment Arus is born Aisha feels a strong attachment to her little nephew and her feelings for him turn romantic as she takes care of him over the years, she tried to suppress her own feelings because she knew Rudeus and Lilia would never approve; Ars confessed he had a crush on her shortly after he turned ten, despite her initial resistance Aisha soon gave in and started a sexual relationship with him; Aisha became pregnant, the two ran off in elopement after being found out by Rudeus; Aisha pointed out to Lillia that she was the one who taught her to find it acceptable to sleep with her own half-brother if he ever showed interest, so Lilia shouldn't find it wrong that Aisha applied those "teachings" to Ars instead, when Lillia was the only one besides Rudeus to find Aisha's actions revolting; like Norn, eventually targeted by Man-God who intends to harm Rudeus's sisters to break him; after Ars grew up and proposed, Aisha married him and hung up her maid uniform for good, signifying she had become a true family member| [Birth: Buena Village, K413] born hours after Norn of Paul and Lillia's affair (violating the Millis Church's strict monogamy vow), raised by Lillia from birth to be Rudeus's personal maid; Lillia taught her to view Zenith as her mistress instead of as another mother, regardless of Zenith's actual feelings, drilled to the point where Aisha maintained a servant's view of Zenith even though Zenith saw herself as Aisha's mother; [Teleport Incident: Shirone Kingdom, Age 4] teleported to the Shirone royal palace with Lillia, raised apart from Rudeus; [Late Timeline: Sharia, Age 10+] acts as an older sister figure to the children of her half-brother Rudeus, helps that she isn't that much older than them; manages the household's domestic operation; [Epilogue] eventually lives with Ars as his wife and raises their son Leroy; Aisha created and used the spell that brought Akito Shinohara to the Six-Faced World}

# Ars Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {First son of Rudeus and Eris; elder half-brother of the later Greyrat children; named after the Great Hero of Humanity Ars, an in-universe story hero; eventually partner to Aisha and father of Leroy; got Aisha pregnant and became a father before he turned 12; there's a 14-year age gap between him and Aisha, and he starts a sexual relationship with her when he's only ten years old| [Infancy: Sharia] clung to Aisha, fussed under Rudeus, obsessed with breasts from infancy as a "Dirty Kid" like his father and grandfather, as a baby cries when picked up by a man, calm in Aisha's arms; [Elopement Incident: Age 10] confessed a crush on his half-aunt Aisha, 14 years his elder, around age ten and asked her to marry him; despite Aisha's initial resistance they started a sexual relationship, Aisha became pregnant, the two ran off in elopement after being found out by Rudeus; [Training: Holy Land of Swords and Sharia, post-Elopement] became a Master Swordsman training with the best swordmasters of his era including Gino Britts, Nina Farion, Isolde, Ghislaine Dedoldia, Alek, and Sandor, among others; since Aisha already excelled at everything else, he wanted to only be her sword, like Eris was for Rudeus, and to do that, he felt he had to surpass his mother Eris; when Arus finally graduated, Rudeus gave his approval for him to make Aisha his wife, but Arus refused until he faced his mother and proved he'd grown into being a man, eventually beating Eris (a Sword King rank) in single combat, proving himself to be among the greatest swordsmen of his age; [Late Timeline] Divine-tier swordsman recognized by Orsted, Kalman III, and the Ogre God Empire swordsmen, eventually titled "Wind God"; [Epilogue] eventually married Aisha after he came of age and proposed| Brave and protective of his siblings, throwing himself between them and kidnappers armed only with a stick; refuses to harm those weaker than him, following his mother Eris's teachings; mischievous and reckless, once getting his siblings lost in Millishion and nearly kidnapped; shares his father and grandfather's Notos family fixation on large breasts; traumatized when his mother Eris cut off his arm after she caught up to him and Aisha post-elopement, this galvanized him to become stronger to prove to his mother he was a man and to earn his father's approval to take Aisha as his wife; Aisha was the only woman he ever wanted to marry; considered a very attractive young man when grown up, the desire of many young girls| First son of Rudeus and Eris; half-nephew and eventual partner to Aisha; father of Leroy; dead ringer for his grandfather Paul, but with bright red wavy hair from Eris's side| Wielded a chantless Wind Magic-integrated Sword God Style that enhanced his speed enough to surpass the one-armed Kalman III, though he never formally claimed the Sword God title; won the combat tournament of the Ogre God Empire; became the greatest swordsman in all of the North; "Ars" or "Arus", multiple acknowledged transliterations| [Infancy: Sharia] clung to Aisha, fussed under Rudeus; [Training under Eris] reached Saint rank in the Sword God Style; [Age 10: Elopement Incident] confessed crush on Aisha, started a sexual relationship, Aisha got pregnant, the two eloped; [Post-Elopement] trained under Eris, Gino, Nina, Isolde, Ghislaine, Alek, Sandor to become a Master Swordsman; [Late Timeline: Sharia] reached his prime as a Divine-tier swordsman, married Aisha after coming of age, lived with her and their son Leroy}

# Ruijerd Superdia

SHORT_DESC: {400+ year old Superd warrior; makes it his life's purpose to restore the honor of the Superd race; over 500 years old but looks like a young man since demon races are Long-Lived; may very well be the last of the Superd race and the only one confirmed to have survived to the present, until Rudeus comes across him again in a Superd village hidden in a forest in Volume 23; [Laplace Era] former war chief of the Superd warrior group serving Laplace; [Post-Laplace through Pre-Story] spent centuries travelling the world to dissolve the Superd stigma, a quest to find if any other Superd survived the war; known as "Dead End" on the Magic Continent and is quite literally the bogeyman of the magic races, as parents warn their children to behave or "Dead End" will get them, towns will summon every guard and adventurer to drive him off if he's spotted; [Age 10-13: Demon Continent: K417-K419] A-rank adventurer nicknamed "Dead End" and "Watch Dog", travels with Rudeus and Eris as family; leaves for parts unknown once the Dead End party finally returns to the Fedoa Region because he believes that Rudy and Eris don't need his protection anymore; he and Rudeus have a brief reunion in Volume 11 as one of Norn and Aisha's escorts, only to again depart the very next day, doesn't reappear until Volume 23; [Academy Era: K419+] paternal escort to young Norn Greyrat on her journey to Sharia, becomes her secret crush and eventually her husband; Ruijerd is a Superd and he marries the human girl Norn in all timelines, hundreds of years younger than him, Ruijerd first met her when she was a child; one of Rudeus's closest friends and becomes his brother-in-law after marrying Norn; between Rudeus and Geese, resolved when Rudeus and Cliff cure his village's plague; [Post-War: Biheiril Kingdom] reunited with surviving Superd in a hidden forest village, possessed by Abyssal King Vita, saved by Cliff; having just barely recovered from the Superd plague, he's not able to fight at his full strength during the final battle though he still provides crucial aid; [Post-War Epilogue] founds and rules the Kingdom of Neris on the Demon Continent as sovereign of a free Superd people| Fierce and terrifying exterior belies a deeply kind, fatherly, protective nature; the manga depicts Ruijerd having comical reactions to some of the antics of his charges; likes giving headpats to children, but Rudeus and Eris are the only ones who would allow him to do that as they aren't scared of him; always helps children no matter what the circumstances, be it rescuing them from kidnappers or leading two of them home across multiple continents; goes berserk against anyone harming a child; a good and virtuous man who wishes to restore his race's reputation, but if someone does evil in front of him, especially harming a child, he'll kill them without mercy; follows a very strict warrior's code and displays many of this trope's personality traits; due to his tragic past, developed a code that anyone he deems villainous is worth slaying regardless of their circumstances; stubborn and adamant sense of justice; refuses to tolerate even minor crimes regardless of social context; widely misunderstood as a monster due to his race's stigma; to a mild degree| [Age 10-13: Demon Continent] swears family loyalty to Rudeus and Eris; reveres Rudeus for his promise to restore the Superd race's honor; early in his journey with Rudeus and Eris, shaves his distinctive green hair to conceal his Superd identity; [Academy Era] tolerates Norn's innocent crush with patient distance; teases Rudeus for not noticing Eris's feelings, asks Rudeus to make an attempt to reconcile with Eris when he has the chance; when the time comes for them to part ways, he praises Rudeus and Eris for how much they've grown as warriors| Honors promises to extremes; protects every child unconditionally; the spear he wields originally belonged to his long-dead son Ruiweld; bears heavy guilt for killing his own son Ruiweld while cursed; the third-eye gemstone on his forehead acts as a radar for unseen enemies and turns blue with ominous markings when possessed by Abyssal King Vita| [Laplace Era] born in the Babinosu Region to a pair of Superd warriors, elected Laplace's Superd war chief within decades; Laplace tricked him and his people into using cursed spears that drove them into a killing frenzy, forever staining their reputation as bloodthirsty savages; Laplace betrayed the Superd with cursed Devil's spears inducing frenzy to slaughter kin; killed his own son Ruiweld, who dying freed him from the trance; struck the fatal blow turning the tide against Laplace; fell in love with Norn during the time in which he fell ill with the Superd plague and she nursed him until he got better; name can be spelled "Ruijierd" depending on the translation; another spelling of his surname is "Sperdia"}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Pre-Story onward] 190cm / 6'3", muscular, broad-shouldered, lean Superd frame, unchanged across centuries due to long Superd lifespan| Penile: [Pre-Story onward] average, intact, ~5"| Skin: porcelain white, smooth throughout| Face: handsome, square-jawed, smooth oval chin; [Laplace Era onward] long scar trailing from right top of forehead across glabella to left cheek| Hair: [Pre-Story through Demon Continent Journey] emerald green, mid-length; [Academy Era onward] shaven bald with a headband covering the forehead gemstone to bypass anti-Superd stigma| Forehead: red gemstone third eye acting as a radar; [Post-War: Biheiril] turns blue with ominous markings when possessed by Abyssal King Vita| Outfit: [Pre-Story onward] brown tribal vest covering back and sides only, exposing frontal torso and arms, never wears a shirt; [Age 10-13: Demon Continent onward] necklace gifted by Rudeus worn around the neck}
