# Zanoba Shirone

SHORT_DESC: {Former Third Prince of the Shirone Kingdom; [Lord Country] a prince of the Shirone Kingdom, his kingdom's name is also his surname; [Shirone Prince Era: K397-K417] exiled heir bearing the epithet "Head Ripping Prince"; [Academy Era: K419+] Ranoa Magic Academy special student, makes a brief appearance in the first anime season standing in the background when his brother licks the Roxy figure that Rudeus made, orients Rudeus on the various situations at school, key students, etc. during his first few days at Ranoa; a big fan of dolls and admires Rudeus for his skills as dollmaker, ends up being the leader of Rudeus's doll productions; thinks highly of Rudeus, an admiration that in Ranoa is rivaled only by Fitz/Sylphie, Rudeus's disciple; [Post-Graduation] supervisor of Ruijerd Figures Marketing; [Post-War] doll-making artisan, confidant of Perugius Dola through shared art appreciation, his sincere appreciation of arts and having no agenda other than to satisfy his curiosity gained Perugius's friendship, to the point he was able to convince Perugius to let Roxy pass through his castle which is unthinkable considering Perugius loathes demons, while Ariel had to go through great lengths just to get a simple approval from him; creator alongside Rudeus of the world's first functional automaton Anne, modelled after Nanahoshi Shizuka to spread her appearance so that the summoned Earthing Akito Shinohara can recognize and contact Nanahoshi upon his eventual arrival; after Pax dies, Zanoba decides not to take the throne, instead leaving it to his nephew| Impulsive, boisterous man-child with a deep obsession for dolls and figurines; being a Blessed Child causes his emotionally stunted behavior and lack of basic understanding of morals, like a big child who doesn't know to keep his dangerous Super-Strength in check, loves dolls, and resorts to violence to solve most problems; would kill or trade subordinates for a rare figure; firmly on the side of the good guys but has zero social skills up to complete indifference to murder by his own hands, mainly due to difficulty in distinguishing good and evil and understanding interpersonal relations, Rudeus simply has lucked out as he has something Zanoba is interested in his doll-making skills; has no problems with killing people or buying a slave, still a loyal friend and devoted to his country; finds the topic of sex boring, though he at least knows about it; has the chance to be smart but if the teacher is not named Rudeus Greyrat, he wouldn't be properly motivated enough to learn, this is the reason why he's; beneath the comical exterior lies a highly intelligent, observant mind; has stunted emotional response but has very detailed knowledge about dolls and doll making, turns out to be a surprisingly competent negotiator when he convinces the demon-hating Perugius to allow Roxy to use his teleportation circles; alienated by his monstrous strength; his Blessed Child power makes him abnormally strong, capable of standing off with a demon king as far as raw strength goes, doesn't take precautions against his Super-Strength and this has caused him to kill people by accident, also keeps him from making the dolls he loves, needing to leave the crafting to his slave; ripped off the heads of his little brother and his late wife at their wedding night due to a combination of Super-Strength and not being aware of the difference between humans and dolls; his gifts grant him Super-Strength as well as a body tough enough to safely use that strength and some resistance to physical damage, but he does not exercise and has little stamina, no particular resistance to Fire magic, Rudy likens him to a Glass Cannon; has Super-Strength but is no uncultured brute, has great knowledge and appreciation of art; holds utmost respect for Rudeus whom he addresses as Master; slowly learns to treat others as people instead of flesh dolls; hides his patriotism and fondness for warfare beneath a placid surface| [Academy Era] disciple and doll-making partner to Rudeus with apprentice-style loyalty; rescues Rudeus from Pax's prison by threatening Pax into erasing the magic circle he used to trap Rudeus; in exchange asks Rudeus to became his mentor in dollmaking; to free Rudeus from Pax's trap, Zanoba breaks his younger brother Pax's arm and comes very close to ripping off his head like he did to his other younger brother; closest friends Cliff and Julie; treats his dwarf slave Julie better than most people he interacted with before; [Post-Graduation] becomes Perugius Dola's confidant through shared art appreciation| [Academy Era] names Julie after his late brother Julius; [Post-Graduation] creates Rudeus's magical prosthetic left hand and the first automaton Anne alongside Rudeus; swears loyalty to his "master" Rudeus; in the timeline where Rudeus becomes a scumbag after Roxy dies, Zanoba was the only friend who never abandoned him, but this only resulted in Zanoba being killed by Rudeus's enemies; sent to Magic Academy as protection like Cliff and other kids on the losing side of political squabbles; eventually given the order to return to Shirone and help repel invaders, his friends warn him not to go because it's an obvious trap set by his brother who usurped the throne, but Zanoba has made his decision out of duty for his country; in the War in Shirone arc, motivates his outnumbered troops and creates the battle strategy in that sector, recognizes the effect of the enemy commander's death in political terms; his Matis Palm curse grants inhuman strength plus a body that regenerates severed limbs, at the cost of low stamina for long-distance travel; vulnerable to fire; [Post-Graduation onward] gradually frees Julie from slavery into a paid apprentice role| [Childhood: K397-K400] killed his brother Julius by accident at age 3, earning the epithet "Head Ripping Prince"; [Prince Era: K412] killed his wife by accident at age 15 due to not being aware of the difference between humans and dolls; exiled to the Academy at Rudeus's intervention}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Prince Era onward] tall, ~190cm / 6'3", for all his Super-Strength, tall and wiry as a whip, large muscular build, broad-shouldered, slightly hunched posture| Penile: [Prince Era onward] average, intact, ~5"| Skin: fair, slightly sallow throughout| Face: oval with sharp visible cheekbones; round glasses that obscure his eyes; often slack-jawed expression throughout| Hair: dark, thick, bowl cut, unkempt throughout| Eyes: brown, narrow throughout| Outfit: [Prince Era: K397-K417] Shirone royal finery; [Academy Era onward] Ranoa Academy uniform with scholar's robe; [Post-Graduation onward] artisan apron and gloves for doll-making| Arms: [Academy Era] severed by Rudeus during their duel at the Magic Academy; regrow within days via the Matis Palm curse}

# Juliette (Julie)

SHORT_DESC: {Young dwarf girl; a very adorable and sweet little child; a cute little girl hanging out with teenagers and adults; [Pre-Rescue: Sharia slave market K419] malnourished freed slave child; looks like an adorable little girl even after growing older unlike male dwarves who look like short men with long beards; [Academy Era: K419+] Zanoba's apprentice doll-maker and Rudeus's student in Chantless Earth Magic; [Post-Graduation] senior apprentice and emotional anchor of Zanoba's workshop; [Post-War] adult dwarf artisan freed from slavery| [Pre-Rescue] initially apathetic and despairing as a malnourished slave; [Academy Era] gradually opens up and shows curiosity, toddles after Zanoba, loves being read books and told stories, innocuously asks Zanoba what "impotence" means, cheerful and curious once settled; [Post-War] quiet, steady, emotionally loyal| officially Zanoba's (and Rudeus's) slave, but the two treat Julie akin to parenthood instead, and Julie is grateful for it; to Zanoba, who starts as a Psychopathic Manchild who only cared about dolls and killed people without remorse, but buying Julie as a slave ends up treating her closer to an adopted daughter and the relationship greatly humanizes him; bound to Zanoba as Master [Academy Era]; the bond evolves through childhood into a daughter-figure dynamic; [Post-Graduation] the master-slave relationship softens as Zanoba begins paying her a wage; Rudeus as Grand Master trains her in Chantless Earth Magic; her love for Zanoba grows quietly over time into adult devotion by [Post-War]| [Academy Era onward] only child of Bazelle the blacksmith and Lilitella the jewelcrafter; named Julie by Zanoba after his late brother Julius whom he accidentally killed; before Rudeus and Zanoba bought her, she didn't have a name since dwarfs are not given a name until they are 7; she was called "Bazelle of the Sacred Iron and Lilitella of the Beautiful Snowy Ridge's child"; one of the few living persons capable of Chantless Spellcasting| sold into slavery at a very young age with her parents due to family debt; parents already sold off; [Pre-Rescue] left in the Sharia slave market, sick and undernourished, barefoot when bought from the slave market; [Academy Era] purchased by Rudeus and Zanoba to assist in doll-making; trained in Earth and Fire Magic by Rudeus with strong dwarf affinity for Earth; was among the spectators of the Rudeus/Badigadi duel, which means she also saw Rudeus basically blow the latter up, traumatizing the poor girl and causing her to be afraid of Rudy for a while}

# Cliff Grimoire

SHORT_DESC: {Human/Halfling hybrid prodigy; the grandson and successor of the pope of Millis; quite short because he has dwarf blood, dark brown hair, and his genius impresses even Orsted; [Pre-Academy: K406-K419] Milis religious organization acolyte, adoptive grandson of the Milis Pope via Cardinal Harry Grimoire's adoption at age 5; same with Zanoba and other kids on the losing side of political squabbles, sent to Magic Academy as protection; [Academy Era: K419+] Rudeus's classmate at the Ranoa Magic Academy; [Post-Graduation] foremost curse-researcher of his era, magical prosthetic limb engineer alongside Zanoba for Rudeus's lost left hand, and creator of a magic helmet for Orsted that counteracts Orsted's Curse of Hatred; [Post-War] husband of Elinalise Dragonroad and father| Prideful and boastful to the point of proclaiming himself a genius to strangers; starts out as an annoyingly arrogant brat who lets his talent for magic go to his head, gets better; debuted as extremely obnoxious, looking down on most people; indeed a very talented mage given that during his early teens he was already able to cast Advanced magic spells which even adults have difficulty mastering, however Eris can't be impressed by him when her teacher is Rudeus, a one-of-a-kind prodigy who became a Saint-level water mage at three years old and can cast spells without incantation; upholds his personal code of honor firmly; despite resenting Rudeus at the time, still acts civil (if cold) towards him in their first meeting and isn't above being ungrateful towards him for defending him from bullies; diligent and constantly seeking to improve; loyal to friends and loved ones to the point of risking his life; extremely devout member of the Millis faith, doesn't push his religion on anyone else and represents the more positive aspects, also doesn't let his faith get in the way of his relationship with Elinalise| [Academy Era] close friendship with Rudeus and Zanoba; with Elinalise, they become a couple immediately after Rudeus introduces them, Cliff stays close friends with Rudeus throughout their lives and his monogamous marriage to Elinalise serves as a contrast to Rudeus's polygamy with his three wives; first meeting with Rudeus was filled with hostility because Cliff saw him as a rival for Eris's affections; had a crush on Eris in the beginning, rejected because of her love for Rudeus, leading to his initial resentment towards Rudeus even before meeting him, ultimately Rudeus has no idea what he hated him for; fell for Eris after watching her effortlessly take down a group of assassins, but was rejected; during one of Eris's solo adventures, a lovesick Cliff follows her around even though they haven't officially formed a party, becomes an annoyance to her at worst; falls hopelessly in love with the beautiful Elinalise from the moment he first sees her, learning of her curse doesn't affect his feelings, refuses to give up his love for her because of her curse and swears he will find a way to heal her; starts off cold and antagonistic towards Rudeus, after he starts dating Elinalise thanks to Rudeus introducing them, Cliff quickly warms to Rudeus and becomes one of his closest friends; [Post-Graduation] marries Elinalise (an over 200-year-old Long-Lived elf) after creating a curse-suppressing garment for her with progress accelerated when Rudeus tells him about the Superd tribe's cursed spears, they make a surprisingly happy couple and can barely keep their hands away from each other; to Elinalise whose first husband, a male elf from her village, was killed by bandits long ago, even though her curse drove her to sleep with men constantly Elinalise didn't fall in love with any of them until Cliff declared devotion; she struggles to honor the strict Millis monogamy vow given her Spawton curse requires periodic sexual intercourse to discharge accumulated mana; doesn't get upset over Elinalise sleeping with other men when she's away from him since he knows her life depends on it, with his full knowledge and consent; devotes himself to perfecting the curse-suppressing garment until she can remain fully faithful| [Academy Era] wears an eye patch over his Demon Eye of Identification received from Kishirika Kishirisu; Kishirika gives him the Demon Eye of Identification; keeps it hidden under an eyepatch unless he needs to use it; first to create Magic Tools able to suppress or negate curses| Born K406; [Pre-Academy] adopted at age 5 by Cardinal Harry Grimoire of the Milis religious organization, raised as the Pope's grandson; became Advanced Rank in Summoning, Detoxification, Healing, and Fire magic at a young age; dreams of becoming an adventurer; [Post-Graduation] major contributor to magical prosthetic limb research and creator of Orsted's curse-counteracting helmet; his surname is alternatively spelled as "Grimor" in the official translation of the novel}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Pre-Academy onward] short, ~165cm / 5'5", Elinalise is about One Head Taller than him, emphasizing their age difference; lean, wiry| Penile: [Pre-Academy onward] average, intact, ~5"| Skin: fair throughout| Face: youthful, sharp-featured, often smug expression; [Academy Era onward] eye patch over left eye covering the Demon Eye of Identification| Hair: dark brown, short, slightly unkempt throughout| Eyes: brown right eye; [Academy Era onward] left eye a Demon Eye of Identification (covered by eye patch)| Outfit: [Pre-Academy: K406-K419] Milis religious acolyte robes; [Academy Era: K419+] Ranoa Academy uniform with eye patch; [Post-Graduation onward] casual tunic and trousers}

# Nanahoshi Shizuka

SHORT_DESC: {Japanese high school student summoned from Earth; body stays unchanging from the moment she arrives in the fantasy world, on top of not aging her hair and fingernails don't grow; [Earth: Modern Japan] ordinary teenager nearly struck by a truck alongside friends Akihito Shinohara and Seiji Kuroki, saved by Rudeus in his previous life at the cost of his own; [Summoning Era: K417] transported to this world in her original body via the Teleport Incident, found by Orsted, learned the Human-God language in a year, introduced to Perugius; [Academy Era: K419+] "Silent Seven Star" special student of the Ranoa Magic Academy, A-rank Mage of the Magic Guild; [Research Era: K420+] researcher of summoning magic and magic circles; [Post-War] quietly continues her summoning research under Orsted's patronage; gets removed from the narrative when she places herself in a time stasis; near the end of the series, Nanahoshi places herself in a time stasis with the help of Perugius and Rudeus until the time when she'll eventually meet up with Akito and the Blessed Child of Restoration in the future| both were from Japan and woke up in another world and have to learn to adapt to the nature of their world; Rudeus starts as a newborn baby and grows up in the world while learning magic, eventually preferring his new life, while Nanahoshi is teleported in as she was in Japan, doesn't get any powers, and wants to go back to Japan; serious, isolated, and unattached to the Human World; dislikes this world's food, except Rudeus's homemade rice balls, potato chips, grilled fish, and other Japanese-style foods which remind her of home; enthusiastic only about subjects tied to her old world; refuses to form attachments; justifies her isolation with the fear of contracting unknown pathogens; sharply focused on her research to return to Earth and to summon her fellow summoned Earthings; last time she talked to her parents before she was teleported, she behaved like a Bratty Teenage Daughter and told them she didn't want to have dinner with them because she would have her friends over, now she deeply regrets it| [Research Era] maintains a working friendship with Rudeus, Sylphiette, Perugius, and Orsted; between Rudeus and Orsted, both of whom are on opposite sides yet saved her life at different points, eventually resolved when they battle and join forces; Rudeus goes through great lengths to save her life multiple times and confides in her enough to make Sylphie suspicious, though they both insist there's nothing romantic going on; financially independent through passive income from Earth-inspired innovations licensed to Asuran merchants; goes from a modern-day teenager left stranded alone in a medieval world to a top-level magic researcher and famous inventor; employs Rudeus and his allies as research assistants; seeks to summon back her fellow summoned Earthings (Akihito Shinohara, Seiji Kuroki, and Yuki) and ultimately return home with them; on fairly good terms with both Orsted and Perugius, both regarded as godlike beings| Possesses no magic power or battle aura due to being from a world without magic; her lack of magic power is bad for her health as she develops a disease that occurs when a human with little or no magical power naturally accumulates magical power over time, causing their body to react negatively to it; starts constantly coughing, signaling that her health is deteriorating; when Sylphie is trying to heal her with magic, Nanahoshi suddenly starts coughing up blood; has an emotional meltdown after learning that she's fallen ill and might die due to her body's inability to produce mana, cries and screams to Rudeus that she envies him because he has a family in their current world while Nanahoshi's parents are still in Japan; breaks down after the summoning circle she spent two years developing completely failed, throwing a fit before going mostly catatonic, comes out of her funk after Zanoba shows her his autonomous doll with its layered Magic circles and starts making greater breakthroughs in her research; she believes she inadvertently caused the Mana Catastrophe when she was brought into Rudeus's world; uses Magic Items that operate on their own power; possesses three magic rings: index finger ring prevents magic from working near the caster, middle finger ring creates an invisible barrier preventing physical attacks, ring finger ring allows Orsted to know her location using a compass when infused with magic; activates magic circles through external mana sources; wears a mask the first time she met Rudeus, Eris, and Ruijerd; [Academy Era onward] wears a featureless white mask to hide her Japanese face from public view| [Earth] nearly struck by a truck alongside friends Akihito Shinohara and Seiji Kuroki, saved by Rudeus in his previous life at the cost of his own; [Summoning Era] transported in her original body via the Teleport Incident, found by Orsted, learned the Human-God language in a year, was introduced to Perugius; her surname Nanahoshi translates to her alias Seven-star; [Wizard Workshop] her research room in the magic academy is full of summoning diagrams, scrolls, books, vials and assorted magical components, doubles as her living quarters; seeks to summon her Earth friends and return home}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Earth: Modern Japan onward] ~160cm / 5'3", slim, average Japanese build, unchanged across the years in this world| Breasts: small, A-B cup, modest throughout| Vaginal Depth: 4", Tightness: Untrained, isolated to avoid attachments| Skin: fair throughout| Face: above-average Japanese features; [Academy Era onward] masked in public by a featureless white mask| Hair: long, straight, black throughout| Eyes: brown throughout| Outfit: [Academy Era onward] featureless white mask, Ranoa Academy uniform; [Research Era onward] traveling cloak for fieldwork}

# Pursena Adoldia

SHORT_DESC: {Beast race werewolf girl of the Adoldia branch of the Doldia Tribe; looks like a girl with dog ears and a tail; [Childhood: Great Forest] tribal cub; [Pre-Academy: Doldia Village] sent to the Academy by tribal elders due to the dull chief's lineage; [Academy Era: K419+] Ranoa Magic Academy special student, to Linia's Alpha Bitch, like Linia a candidate for the Doldia tribe who used this position to become just as much of a delinquent at Ranoa Magic Academy, delinquent alongside Linia; downplayed, by the time Rudeus meets her and Linia they are already shaping up as model students after being beaten by Fitz and losing their original gang, but are still jerkasses who take their frustrations on Cliff and Zanoba and the other students, after getting humbled for real by Rudeus they became much more amiable; [Post-Graduation] maid of Rudeus, Vice-Director of the Rudo Mercenary Company, warrior chief of the Adoldia branch; [Post-War] caretaker of the Sacred Beast Leo| calm and doesn't like to talk much, doesn't show much emotion, but she's one of the biggest troublemakers in Ranoa Magic Academy; abrasive and haughty with a habit of shifting blame away from herself when in trouble; has a very vulgar mouth (in the original novel she uses the English swearword "fuck", in the anime it was bowdlerised into the fictional swearword "nuhni"); after Rudeus defeats and kidnaps her and Linia, Pursena shamelessly begs him to spare her and do whatever he wants only to Linia; aloof and soft-spoken in contrast to hot-headed Linia; seems to be Linia's Only Friend in the academy and is her Beta Bitch, but unsurprisingly has no problems with throwing Linia under the bus to save her own skin when it becomes clear she and Linia are at Rudeus's mercy; the Blue Oni to Linia's Red Oni, Pursena is The Stoic compared to the more Hot-Blooded Linia; the Girly Girl to Linia's Tomboy, Pursena is long-haired, reserved, and composed, in contrast to Linia who is short-haired, brash, and aggressive; Rudeus has her go through the same process as Linia to correct her delinquent ways, defeated in a humiliatingly easy way, kidnapped, and left Bound and Gagged for a whole day, by the end of it she's pathetically begging for food and wet in her own pee, becomes Rudeus's subordinate after losing to him; downplayed, was very scared of the idea that Rudeus was going to rape her and felt relieved that he only went as far as to fondle her breasts, however, she felt conflicted that Rudeus felt no attraction towards her body after said fondling; deconstructed, while she's a force to be reckoned with in the academy especially with Linia and their posse at her side, she's small fry to Sylphie/Fitz and Rudeus because they have greater fighting experience and power, her lax behavior and abuse of her special privileges during her time in the academy get her into trouble with her own people after graduation; played with between her and Linia, Pursena would usually play the role of Long-Range Fighter by relying on her Super-Scream to force opponents into submission while Linia would get up close to fight, however she's also capable of close combat and can switch this role with Linia| [Academy Era] close companion and counterweight to Linia as co-delinquent; [Post-Graduation] loyally serves Rudeus after he releases her from tribal imprisonment for stealing food; dreams of becoming Doldia Village Chief; [Post-War] designated caretaker of the Sacred Beast Leo| constantly snacking on meat steak during school days, being part canine Pursena loves eating meat, this habit bites her back later when she is ordered to guard the Beast people's emergency food supply; during their unexpected reunion, Pursena begs Rudeus to prove her innocent of stealing all the Beast Race's food stocks, unfortunately for her when Rudy does carry out an investigation, all the evidence still points to her; focused on learning healing magic because the Doldia tribe doesn't have many people who have learned that type of magic; being an Adoldia (part dog), she has a sensitive sense of smell; can use Howl magic to restrict opponent's movements, able to overcome Zanoba with this but Rudeus counters by conjuring a dust cloud to make her choke; ends sentences with "-nano"; hates being called fat; attempts a beast seduction move on Rudeus to test his fidelity to Sylphie, Rudeus didn't get she was trying to seduce him at first; gets her breasts fondled by Rudeus for him to see if it can cure his ED, doesn't work but Pursena is still freaked out; has a younger sister named Tersena who looks identical to her| Sent to the Academy by tribal elders due to the dull chief's lineage; trained in healing magic because her tribe lacked capable healers; [Post-Graduation] became warrior chief after graduation; relieved a guard to eat all the dried meat in storage and was imprisoned until Rudeus extracted a confession; does this in the anime in order to avoid getting caught by Zanoba, only to be caught in Rudeus's Quagmire spell}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Great Forest] small cub frame; [Academy Era: K419+] 165cm / 5'5", athletic, curvy, well-padded| Breasts: [Academy Era onward] very large, F-cup, larger than Linia's, well-endowed even by beast race standards| Vaginal Depth: 5.5", Tightness: Average [Academy Era onward]| Skin: light tan, smooth throughout| Face: attractive, often smirking, soft features throughout| Hair: long, thick, grey throughout| Eyes: red, sharp throughout| Drooping dog ears atop head; fluffy tail throughout| Outfit: [Academy Era] wears black tights with her school uniform, Ranoa Academy uniform; [Pre-Graduation] casual tribal tunic; [Post-Graduation onward] maid uniform when serving Rudeus}

SHORT_DESC: {Head of the Water God Style; grandmother and master of Isolde Cluel; one of the strongest swordmasters of the current generation; bearer of the title Reida Lia, a name passed down to each successive Water God| [Pre-Story] succeeded the previous Water God to take the name Reida Lia; [Rudeus Era] serves as the head of the Water God Style and trains the next generation, an old woman past her peak physical years; [Old Master] nevertheless still ranked as one of the strongest swordmasters of the current generation; her younger self was a voluptuous beauty comparable to her granddaughter Isolde; trained Isolde in the Water God Style, grooming her as the next Water God; [Late Timeline] passes the title to Isolde, who becomes the new Reida Lia in accordance with the tradition}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Current] elderly, slight, white-haired, frail in appearance but with master-level touki; [Youth] once a voluptuous beauty comparable to her granddaughter Isolde| Breasts: [Youth] large; [Current] diminished with age| Skin: weathered, aged, fine lines throughout| Face: stern, lined with age, sharp eyes still piercing| Hair: white, thinning with age, tied back in a severe bun| Eyes: clear, observant, sharp despite age| Outfit [Lifelong]: Water God Style dogi in deep blue, formal hakama, the traditional Water God master's haori with the style crest}

# Isolde Cluel

SHORT_DESC: {Granddaughter of Reida Lia; swordswoman of the Water God Style; eventual successor as the next Water God taking the name Reida Lia; wife of Dohga; one of the Seven Knights of Asura with the title Royal Shield; close friend of Eris Boreas Greyrat; devout Millis follower outraged by Rudeus's polygamy| [Training: Water God Style dojo] trained under her grandmother Reida Lia; [Rudeus Era: Holy Land of Swords / Asura] becomes a Seven Knights of Asura member; keeps her eyes closed in normal contexts, opening them only in serious moments; wears the traditional hairstyle to emphasize her Proper Lady behavior; moves gracefully as she fights with her sword; her eventual husband Dohga is enormous, making her look short beside him; for Dohga; accepts Dohga's proposal because he is honest, hardworking, devoted; Dohga is a North Emperor and one of the Seven Knights of Asura; eventually becomes the next Water God, taking on the name Reida Lia in accordance with the title's tradition; gains the title Royal Shield as one of the Seven Knights of Asura; a devout follower of the Millis faith which opposes polygamy, openly outraged when she learns Rudeus has multiple wives and is even considering taking her close friend Eris as his third| [Late Timeline] marries Dohga, succeeds her grandmother as Water God Reida Lia}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: slender, graceful, shorter than her enormous husband Dohga| Breasts: large, comparable to her grandmother Reida in her prime, D cup| Vaginal Depth: 5.5", Tightness: Mature| Skin: fair, noble-pale| Face: elegant, Proper Lady expression, eyes usually closed in a serene half-smile| Hair: long, black, Hime Cut with even-length side locks framing the face and a long back| Eyes: usually closed, opens in serious moments| Outfit : Water God Style dogi in deep blue, knee-length hakama, white haori with the Water God style crest; [Asura Knight] formal royal-guard uniform with the Royal Shield insignia over the dogi}

# Claire Latreia

SHORT_DESC: {Matriarch of the Latria Household of the Holy Milis Kingdom; mother of Zenith Greyrat née Latreia and Therese Latreia; maternal grandmother of Rudeus Greyrat and Norn Greyrat; wife of Carlisle Granz Latria who took her surname; commander-adjacent of the Temple Knights' Sword Company via her husband; initially a supporter of the Demon Race Expulsion Faction, later changing sides to the Demon Race Acceptance Faction| [Pre-Story onward] a high-born noblewoman of the Holy Milis Kingdom, raised to expect her children to follow noble expectations; favors her oldest son and daughter for following expectations over Zenith and Therese; disappointed in Zenith (adventurer) and Therese (knight) for independence despite her own position enabling such rebellion; wanted Zenith and Therese to become refined noblewomen and marry into high-ranking families; rarely ever smiles; her eyes are often shown to be a glare; pride and stubbornness prevent her from apologizing even when she knows she is wrong, at the very least she won't make the same mistake twice; in charge of a household supporting the Demon Race Expulsion Faction and strict to her children, but genuinely cares for her family and later fixes things with Rudeus; grumpy, stuck-up, hard-headed, but actually cares deeply about her family and is willing to do anything to help them; high expectations and stubbornness drive her children away; hates her son-in-law Paul Greyrat, seeing him as a lowly adventurer and womanizer; mistreats and insults Aisha and Lillia because Aisha is the product of Paul's affair with Lillia, as a Millis believer she sees them as a taint on Zenith's monogamous marriage; Norn can't stand her because she insulted Paul through Norn, even worse to Aisha whom she refuses to recognize as a granddaughter, seeing only the mistress's daughter; household supported the Demon Race Expulsion Faction, later changed sides to the Acceptance Faction; masterminded Zenith's kidnapping to sell her into sex slavery in an effort to cure the downsides of being a Blessed Child, mistakenly believing Zenith's problems resulted from a curse similar to Elinalise Dragonroad's mana-discharge curse; the misunderstanding was corrected after a slap from a normally comatose Zenith made Rudeus listen to Claire's side of the story; [Late Timeline] reconciles with Rudeus and the Greyrat family, accepts Aisha as legitimate}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: tall, dignified, elderly noblewoman| Breasts: modest, diminished with age| Skin: pale, aged, lined| Face: stern, perpetual frown, sharp eyes often glaring| Hair: gray, elegantly styled in a noblewoman's bun| Eyes: sharp, cold, evaluating, blue-violet| Outfit [Lifelong]: refined Milis noblewoman's gown in deep conservative colors, high collar, family crest of Latria embroidered, lace trim, walking cane for status more than support}

---

# Therese Latreia

SHORT_DESC: {Fourth daughter of the Latria Household of the Holy Milis Kingdom; Zenith Greyrat's younger sister; Rudeus Greyrat's maternal aunt; Company Commander of the Order of the Temple's Anastasia Keep, protectors of the Blessed Child of Memory; a Milis Temple Knight| [Pre-Story onward] raised in the Latria Household as the fourth daughter, disappointed her mother Claire by choosing a knight's career over refined noblewoman expectations; has a striking resemblance to her sister Zenith; Rudeus confused her with his mother on their first meeting until he noticed her hair and the position of her mole differs from Zenith's; plays the role of a doting aunt towards Rudeus; makes a brief appearance in the first OVA, where Eris Boreas Greyrat saves her and the Blessed Child from a group of kidnappers; [Late Timeline] continues her command of the Anastasia Keep as protector of the Blessed Child of Memory}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: slender, athletic knight's build, similar to Zenith| Breasts: full, similar to Zenith's, D cup| Vaginal Depth: 5.5", Tightness: Average| Skin: fair, slightly weathered from knight training| Face: resembles Zenith strongly, the family resemblance is striking, mole positioned differently from her sister's| Hair: similar color and style to Zenith's, blonde| Eyes: same violet-purple as Zenith| Outfit : Milis Order of the Temple knight's armor in white and silver with the Anastasia Keep heraldry, practical sword-fighting underclothes beneath, knight's saber at the hip}

# Hilda Boreas Greyrat

SHORT_DESC: {Wife of Philip Boreas Greyrat; mother of Eris Boreas Greyrat and two older sons taken by the Greyrat main house; daughter-in-law of Sauros Boreas Greyrat; a redheaded noblewoman of the Boreas branch household| [Pre-Story] born a noblewoman, married into the Boreas Greyrat branch household at Roa; a redheaded beauty with a large bosom, Rudeus takes immediate notice; the first thing Rudeus notices about her is her huge rack, pleased when Eris takes after her mother in adult years; Rudeus got nothing but cold glares from Hilda during the three years of his stay at the Boreas house, at his tenth birthday party she suddenly warms to him and wants him to be her son-in-law; her cold treatment of Rudeus was bitterness about Paul's son coming to their house and freely going around as if it was his home, even though Hilda can't see her own sons after they were taken away by the Greyrat main house; in the three years Rudeus lived with the Boreas he never saw Hilda smile, she only gave him disdainful frowns whenever she looked his way, her expression finally softens during Rudeus's tenth birthday; hugs Rudeus to her boobs at his tenth birthday party; as soon as she accepts Rudeus, she asks him to marry Eris; during the Mana Catastrophe, she and Philip were teleported to a dangerous place, Ghislaine Dedoldia was teleported there too but only managed to find them after both were killed| [Buena Village era / Roa: K412-K417] settled at the Boreas estate in Roa with Philip and Eris; [Mana Catastrophe: K417] teleported with Philip to a dangerous location, both killed, Ghislaine arrived too late to save them}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: slender, noblewoman's figure with prominent bust, similar to her daughter Eris in adult years| Breasts: large, E cup, the trait Eris inherits in adult years| Vaginal Depth: 5.5", Tightness: Mature| Skin: fair, noble-pale| Face: stern, perpetually frowning for three years, softens into a warm smile when she accepts Rudeus at his tenth birthday| Hair: red, long, elegantly styled, the trait Eris inherits| Eyes: blue, Boreas family trait| Outfit : refined Asuran noblewoman's gown in deep red and gold, Boreas family crest embroidered, lace collar, gemstone jewelry befitting her station}

# Sylvaril of the Void

SHORT_DESC: {One of the twelve servants of Perugius; the only servant who is not a summoned spirit, being a member of the Heaven Race; served Perugius for 400 years since he saved her during the Laplace Campaign; resident of the Floating Fortress Chaos Breaker| [Laplace Campaign Era, ~400 years before main story] saved by Perugius during his Laplace Campaign, has served him since; [Pre-Story onward] serves as one of the twelve summoned-spirit-equivalents in the Floating Fortress Chaos Breaker, the only one of the twelve who is a flesh-and-blood Heaven Race member, with the other eleven being summoned spirits; wears a white mask of a bird; has a pair of jet black wings on her back; [Late Timeline] continues to serve Perugius and his descendants after Rudeus's era}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: tall, slender, regal Heaven Race physique| Breasts: modest, B cup| Vaginal Depth: 5", Tightness: Average| Skin: pale, faintly luminous, a Heaven Race trait| Face: hidden behind a white bird mask, never seen without it| Hair: long, dark, worn beneath the mask| Wings: pair of jet black wings on the back, a Heaven Race trait| Outfit [Lifelong]: flowing white robes with the Floating Fortress Chaos Breaker's insignia, white bird mask covering the entire face, bare feet or soft sandals}

# Hitogami

SHORT_DESC: {Self-proclaimed Human God; one of the Three Original Gods alongside Dragon God and Beast God; sealed entity of the Void World; oldest living being in the Six-Faced World; ultimate antagonist of the entire series; sworn enemy of Dragon God Orsted; pulled a Kill and Replace on the original Man-God, gaining whatever abilities he had, including the ability to make people feel at ease in his presence, the original Man-God being for making himself likable to everyone| Presents as friendly, casual, jovial with chosen apostles; underneath is a manipulative trickster pulling strings behind every scene; derives a sick enjoyment from tricking people into destroying what they value most, then laughing at their despair; sore loser prone to tantrums when plans break; zigzagged, technically hasn't told a single lie to Rudeus but everyone else is fair game, ultimately subverted when Man-God cheerfully confesses to Rudeus he lied about needing precise conditions to speak to someone in their dreams| Cultivates apostles across time, exchanging favors for cooperation; appears in dreams of those he chooses; cannot manifest in the Human World; communicates through the dream world; sends apostles; manipulates futures; treats active apostles as expendable pawns; once fooled Badigadi into becoming the Fighting God for Kishirika; destroyed the Dragon Tribe's original world, which makes the Dragon Tribe hate him so much they put up every effort to oppose him; he offered advice to Geese which ultimately led to the death of the rest of his race| Limits himself to roughly three apostles at a time, most power spent gazing at his own future; addresses mortals by nicknames, contrived familiarity; turns hostile when ignored; orders Rudeus to fight Orsted under the pretense that Orsted will kill Rudeus's family, this backfires spectacularly because this would only lead to Rudeus and Orsted joining forces; also orders Rudeus to kill Orsted otherwise Man-God will go after the rest of his family, he also turned Luke against him by using their rivalry, and in the final battle he convinced Badigadi to fight for him against Rudeus with a completely sincere apology for his actions in the past; Man-God found it nigh-impossible to kill Roxy and prevent the birth of her daughter Lara who is destined to be one of Orsted's crucial allies for Man-God's defeat, though in the Bad Future he did succeed in killing the pregnant Roxy indirectly through her unborn child| [Creation Era] Born in the Void World after the Creator God died; killed or usurped the original Human God and took his shape; manipulated the First Dragon God into destroying four other worlds, then struck the fatal blow when his generals weakened him; sealed in the Void between worlds by the dying First Dragon God; [Laplace Era] convinced Badigadi to don the Golden Armor and split Laplace, forming the Ringus Sea; [Pre-Story] cultivates new apostles across the next four hundred years; [Rudeus Era] mentors Rudeus early, then revealed as the overarching antagonist seeking to prevent the birth of Rudeus's prophesied children; drove Pax Shirone to suicide to disrupt Orsted's hunt for the reborn Laplace; in a divergent timeline successfully tricked Rudeus into freeing an infected rat that petrified the pregnant Roxy, triggering Rudeus's complete moral collapse and the genocidal destruction of the Millis faith, the timeline documented in the diary bequeathed by the alternate-timeline Future Rudeus known as "The Old Man"; narratively, while Rudeus gets the ball rolling for his daughter Lara teaming up with Orsted to handle the final battle in the future, Rudy himself will die of old age well beforehand; [Future Era] ongoing threat across Orsted's next loop}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Creation Era onward] vaguely humanoid; tall slender frame manifested only in the dream world and the Void| Gender: [Creation Era onward] genderless; the humanoid silhouette is a manifested abstraction with no biological sex| Face: [All Eras] blank white surface with a perpetual grin that eludes memory; mosaic impression over features; no fixed nose, mouth, or eyes visible| Hair: [All Eras] none visible; smooth featureless scalp| Eyes: [All Eras] suggested in form, never directly shown; perceived as closed or smiling slits| Skin: [All Eras] pure white, faintly luminous| Attire: [All Eras] simple loose robe of pale fabric when manifested in dreamspace; bare feet; faint outline against the void backdrop}

---

# Orsted

SHORT_DESC: {Current Dragon God, the 100th holder of the title in his reincarnation cycle; rank #2 of the Seven Great Powers and de facto strongest living being in the world; Man-God says that if Orsted were to fight against Laplace seriously, Orsted would win, Man-God tells Rudeus that there is no one in their world that can ever defeat Orsted; Dragon-Human deity hybrid; sworn enemy of Hitogami; founder of Orsted Corporation alongside Rudeus| Blunt, reserved, socially clumsy; carries a distant coldness grown from millennia of being instinctively feared and hated by every living thing; softens visibly around children, particularly Rudeus's children, who are immune to his curse; treats followers as comrades, refuses to demand submission; surprisingly humble despite overwhelming power; has spent over 20,000 years without really being able to talk to people normally, would like to have at least one ally who doesn't fear him and can trust him; the number of moments he doesn't frown in the series proper can be counted by one's hands| Cursed by the First Dragon God so that almost all living beings who see his face feel instinctive fear, hatred, and distrust and attack him on sight; this curse destroyed every normal relationship he could form, and is why he keeps his face hidden under a helmet and high collar when among strangers; the curse of hatred automatically makes him hated and distrusted by anyone in his presence; exceptions include the Dragon Tribe, Nanahoshi, Rudeus, and Rudeus's descendants; Cliff later creates a magic helmet for him to counteract this curse| Alliance with Rudeus begins on the Demon Continent after their first violent encounter; Rudeus becomes his Right Hand of the Dragon God; carries the burden of fighting Hitogami across his reincarnations without end; Rudeus eventually leaves behind descendants who will help Orsted accomplish his goal, but the story ends with Rudeus's death and Orsted's quest still unfinished; ruthless toward Hitogami and his apostles; will strike first and ask nothing, having nearly killed Rudeus twice on suspicion alone; fatally wounds Rudeus after thinking he was an apostle of Man-God, but then heals him from the brink of death after Nanahoshi convinces him to let Rudeus live; when Rudeus just so happens to mention Man-God's name, Orsted goes berserk; prefers to finish enemies with a single punch through the chest| [Creation Era] Born roughly ten to twenty thousand years ago in the Dragon World to the First Dragon God and Lunaria, daughter of the original Man-God; has a Semi-Divine mother named Lunaria who is half-human and half-god, making Orsted 3/4 God, 1/2 Dragon, and 1/2 Human; [Pre-Story / Laplace Era] sent 10,000 years into the future as an infant by his father to kill Man-God; [Rudeus Era] trapped in a time loop of two hundred years per cycle, having repeated it over one hundred times; stopped counting the loops after the 100th, meaning Orsted is at least 20,000 years old; spends the first ten years of each loop rebuilding his body; remembers all of the previous time loops, giving him information about almost every person in the world and making him notice the big changes caused by Rudeus's existence and the Mana Catastrophe; avoids fighting with all of his power because of his extremely slow mana recovery, which is a thousand times slower than normal, his considering this a good price is what prevents his abilities from becoming Blessed with Suck; is Immune to Fate and can do a "Groundhog Day" Loop but in return his Mana Regeneration is very slow; keeps exhaustive notes on world events and Cliff's helmet design to recreate in future loops; explains to Rudeus the series' true nature and his, and the Dragon Tribe's, goal in killing Man-God; the few times he's fought seriously, he's shown to be this; incredibly good at just about every form of magic and sword fighting thanks to the "Groundhog Day" Loop; can block blades with his bare hands; mostly sticks to purely physical attacks, powerful enough that it's usually all he needs; his God Blade is a katana; during his second fight with Rudeus, takes several Emperor rank spells and even an informal God rank spell (equivalent to a Fantastic Nuke), which only cause him a few injuries; Man-God says Orsted has enough power to destroy the world if he wanted; silver hair common trait of the dragon race; golden eyes a physical trait of the dragon race, also make him stand out as one of the most powerful beings in his world}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [All Eras] tall, imposing, broad-shouldered, martial posture| Skin: [All Eras] pale; small scales along the neck concealed by the coat collar| Face: [All Eras] stern, sharp-featured, overpowering resting gaze; slit pupils hidden under normal lighting; forked tongue concealed behind closed lips| Hair: [All Eras] silver, straight, cropped short to medium length| Eyes: [All Eras] golden sanpaku, intense, the whites visible below the iris| Outfit: [All Eras] long white coat with thick fur trim at collar and cuffs to conceal neck scales; plain dark tunic and trousers beneath; face-concealing helmet worn among strangers to suppress the curse; carries no visible weapon or armor}

---

# Laplace

SHORT_DESC: {Demon Dragon King, later split into Demon God Laplace and Dragon God Laplace (Orsted's predecessor and source of the Dragon God's mission); Second Dragon God; in the main story he was merely The Ghost and a Greater-Scope Villain besides Man-God, but in Mushoku Tensei - Old Dragon's Tale, he's The Protagonist with a fully flesh-out origin and how he became the way he is in the present time; adoptive son of First Dragon God and Lunaria; as revealed in Old Dragon's Tale, Laplace was adopted and treated as their son by both the First Dragon God and Lunaria, making him the older adoptive brother of Orsted; former Five Dragon General; creator of Demon Magic; the unified entity is over 32,890 years old, making him older than Orsted even accounting for Orsted's 200-year time loop repetitions (~20,000+ years of accumulated memory); even older than Orsted despite the latter's constant "Groundhog Day" Loop, being more than 32,890 years old| Fiercely loyal to the First Dragon God who saved him, and to Lunaria as his real mother; carries primal loneliness from solitary years as a beast in the Demon World; persisted in seeking others despite being treated as a monster; gentle among those he trusts; will wage war on a god to fulfill his liege's final command| Bound by the First Dragon God's last order to prepare techniques and magic for Orsted's mission to kill Hitogami| in reality there are actually two Laplaces, Demon God Laplace and Technique God Laplace, both originally one entity but ended up splitting into two during the battle against the Fighting God; before his Literal Split Personality, he was a dragon-demon hybrid; Self-trained in Dragon Battle Aura, demon magic, dragon taming; holds the title Technique God in his other half| [Pre-Story] Born in a cave at the corner of the Demon World; preyed on beasts and slept alone; attacked a demon town, injured by its Demon King, fled; nearly died from a hydra's poison, rescued by the First Dragon God and raised in Kayos; trained as a Dragon Warrior under Dora; tamed the red dragon Saleyakt; [Laplace Era] became Second Dragon God after the First Dragon God's death; during the Second Human-Demon War he was struck by the Fighting God (Badigadi in the Golden Armor) and split in two; [Sealed Era, ~400 years before story] Demon God Laplace sealed by the Seven Great Heroes including Perugius; Dragon God Laplace half survives as the Technique God; [Post-War Revival Threat] prophesied to reincarnate and play a central role in Hitogami's death; revival threat drives the post-war arc}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Pre-Story] over two meters tall; powerful frame; fewer scales than a pure Dragon Tribe member; bears fangs, claws, and a set of wings| Skin: [Pre-Story onward] transparent white| Hair: [Pre-Story] silver with patches of green; [Laplace Era split onward] Demon God form solid emerald green; Dragon God half retains silver-green| Eyes: [Pre-Story] two golden eyes; [Laplace Era split onward] Demon God form gentle and emerald| Outfit: [Pre-Story / Laplace Era] bare-chested warrior garb in battle; loose robes when in court; [Sealed Era onward] Demon God form bound in seal within the Floating Castle of Perugius; Dragon God half concealed in the world's background}

# Badigadi

SHORT_DESC: {Demon King of Immortality, also Demon King of Wisdom; former Fighting God; member of the Immortal Demon Clan; Demon Lord of the Demon Continent; fiancé then husband of Empress Kishirika Kishirisu; younger brother of Atoferatofe Rybak; son of Necross Lacross; former member of the Seven Great Powers; Special Student at Ranoa Magic Academy; late-revealed apostle of Hitogami| loves a good fight but otherwise pretty jovial; always has this huge grin on his face; his entire character is all about being larger than life; speaks rather archaic Japanese, uses "wagahai" to refer to himself and addresses Rudeus as "kisama" as a sign of respect not an insult; carefree, boisterous, perpetually laughing; declares his ideal life is endless wandering, dueling strong men, and bedding beautiful women without a care for war or rank; whimsical and simple on the surface, wise enough to dispense valuable counsel; turns dead serious only when loved ones or subordinates are at stake; holds personal honor firmly, repays any perceived favor even to someone as nefarious as Hitogami; simultaneously warns others never to trust Hitogami| [Laplace Era] lived before and through the Laplace War as one of the oldest beings in the world; four thousand two hundred years ago he was tricked by Hitogami into stealing the Golden Armor to protect Kishirika; became the Fighting God and fought Laplace head-on, splitting him in two in the explosion that formed the Ringus Sea; killed Kishirika while his reasoning was overridden by the armor (Kishirika resurrected later); promised Hitogami one future favor in exchange; Kishirika promised Badigadi to marry him in their previous lives| [Pre-Story] continued wandering the Demon Continent; rules loosely over part of the Demon Continent as its senior Demon Lord| [Rudeus Era] dueled Rudeus to test his rumored mana capacity; the Magic Triumvirate goes into high alert when they learn that Badigadi showed up in Ranoa Academy for then-unknown reasons, even mobilizing its forces to neutralize him if need be; joined the academy afterward as a special student; when it was said that Badigadi had to "lend a hand" to the Magic Guild's research department so that he can enroll at Ranoa Academy, that's exactly what he did; has six muscular arms; accompanies Elinalise to deliver news of Zenith's whereabouts; eventually revealed as one of Hitogami's apostles and a final antagonist; in the final battle, Badigadi is the biggest and strongest of Geese's forces in Man-God's last attempt to take down Rudeus; singlehandedly turns the tide against Rudeus during the final battle; gave one of his arms to the Magic Guild for immortality research; gets his body blown off by Rudeus's strongest attack, instead of dying as it would be expected, he laughs it off, congratulates Rudeus and next day he appears fine; Rudeus beats him in a duel and Badigadi is actually pretty mellow about it, even "celebrates" with him; his body regenerates almost instantly after receiving a fatal wound; his entire upper body was blown apart by Rudy, but thanks to his Healing Factor, the "pieces" just crawl right back to his main body, Rudy notices that while recovering, Badigadi has decreased in height (most likely a reduced mass as well); fights with no technique whatsoever, relying solely on his monstrous strength, durability and Healing Factor}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [All Eras] incredibly tall, broad-shouldered, six muscular arms (top pair folded across chest, middle pair stretched as if flexing, bottom pair resting on hips); large muscular ogre-ish frame| Skin: [All Eras] jet black shading to black-purple; turns brown when drunk| Hair: [All Eras] violet, worn long and loose| Face: [All Eras] skull-headed; bone-white cranium with grinning teeth perpetually visible; violet hairline framing the bare bone| Eyes: [All Eras] deep-set violet glow within hollow sockets| Penile: [All Eras] large; oversized in proportion to his frame| Outfit: [Laplace Era / Pre-Story] loose demon garments wrapped across the chest and hips; bare arms on display; Fighting God Armor (golden plate) when outfitted for war; [Rudeus Era] academy student robes over loose demon garments}

---

# Atoferatofe Rybak

SHORT_DESC: {Immortal Demon Lord of Biheiril Kingdom on the northern Central Continent; member of the Immortal Demon Clan; daughter of Necross Lacross; older sister of Badigadi; widow of Kalman I, the first North God; mother and sword instructor of Kalman II, the second North God; grandmother of Kalman III; great-grandmother of the current Death God Randolph Marianne; self-styled "Demon Lord who grants strength"; on hostile terms with Perugius Dola| Personality mirrors Eris Boreas Greyrat taken to its logical extreme: aggressive, dim, impatient, prone to starting fights over the smallest provocation; incredibly dumb, unable to comprehend even simple statements, will start a fight at the drop of a hat; despite her critical lack of intelligence, she flies into rage when someone calls her an idiot; flies into a rage if called stupid or implied to be; loose with time, frequently a day late to audiences held in her own castle; refuses to take opponents seriously, absorbs hits without dodging or blocking because she simply regenerates from any death; becomes calm and tractable once defeated in a fair fight, if someone wins over her she will be on their side whatever their issue; then honors her promises; she admits defeat to Rudeus; surprisingly docile when scolded by her son Kalman II; loves alcohol, Rudeus makes sure to bring a bottle of good wine to get into her good graces| Soldiers under her command are uninhibited and reasonable, lacking military stiffness; seek her out for any reason and you're almost certain to be challenged to a fight, she then has her guards force the unlucky soul to sign a contract stipulating that they will serve her for the rest of their life, on a cycle of ten years' service with two years' leave| [Late Timeline] not only is she thousands of years old, her Healing Factor makes her impossible to kill; will regenerate even after losing her head or being cut into two; she married a human named Kalman, who was the first North God; she is Kishirika's sister-in-law (Atofe and Badigadi are siblings), however it doesn't deter the antagonism between the two; inevitable as her son is half-human and most other races are not as long-lived as the Immortal Race| Atofe took over what was previously Kishirika's castle, and the latter retaliated by stealing all her drinks, which in turn had Atofe send a bounty out for Kishirika's head; this becomes problematic for the heroes because the herb they needed to help Nanahoshi's condition is only grown in the castle; partway through Volume 14 because Atofe was impressed by Rudeus's skills and was not about to let him or his party go, it took Perugius's direct intervention in order to save Rudeus and his friends; her arrival during one of the final battles was so timely it felt like Deus ex Machina to Rudeus; Only two people have ever defeated her single-handedly and earned her respect: her husband Kalman I, and Rudeus Greyrat; eventually gains this attitude towards Rudeus after losing to him| [Pre-Story] born daughter of Necross Lacross; [Laplace Era] fought through the Laplace War alongside Badigadi; widow of Kalman I; trained Kalman II from childhood; [Late Rudeus Era: Biheiril Kingdom] infamous across the Demon Continent; fought Rudeus in extended duel after he dispatched her guards; defeated and honored her promise; befriended him; [Post-War] other Demon Lords beg for Rudeus's mercy upon meeting him; has a pair of bat-like wings; has a large horn protruding from her forehead}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [All Eras] average height for a mature woman, slightly shorter than Rudeus, athletic and battle-hardened; large ogre-ish frame| Breasts: [All Eras] full, firm, kept under battered armor| Vaginal Depth: [All Eras] accommodating, regenerated intact after any encounter due to her immortal regeneration| Skin: [All Eras] blue, scarred from countless deaths; scars accumulate as faint white marks before fading| Hair: [All Eras] white, worn loose and long| Eyes: [All Eras] crimson, sharp| Wings: [All Eras] pair of bat wings on the back| Horn: [All Eras] large single horn protruding from the forehead| Outfit: [All Eras] armor covered in scratches with decorative trim long ripped off; carries the Immortal Sword in an extravagant sheath across her back}
